- **Price join:** each site joins its OWN `bidding_zone` through the shared `repo/PriceSlots` series (see the Historie section for the why and the equality proof - it replaced the per-bucket lateral this class ran FIVE times per request), PT15M preferred over PT60M for the same hour, PT60M fallback otherwise. Windows are Europe/Berlin calendar periods (`HistoryRange`; `week` is deliberately not offered); `range=all` starts at the fleet's first covered slot and the response `from` says so. RLS is the tenant fence - no tenant predicate anywhere.
- **`GET /api/v1/earnings?range=day|month|year|all&at=` (default month - captain decision).** Per site + totals: `{baselineEur, actualEur, savedEur, coveredSlots, firstCoveredDate}`; a site with no computable slot gets `savedEur: null` + machine-readable `reason` (`no_data` = no rollup buckets; `missing_channels` = buckets lack load/PV/grid channels, e.g. generation-only Deye string/micro inverters; `no_prices` = measured slots but no price coverage). Each site also carries `dailySaved` (realized savings per Berlin day, last 14 days, independent of range) - feeds the hero spark bars AND the per-site "Heute +X €" teaser in one round-trip. Rollups refresh every 15 min, so the numbers trail live accordingly (fine for money; don't make it tick).
- **Portal:** `EarningsHero` (`components/FleetOverview.tsx`, replaces the Phase-1 `FleetHero`) renders the measured number with count-up, the two-number proof line, the `.vp-fleet-seg` range switch, realized spark bars and the fine print ("zzgl. Marktprämie" for fleets with DV - the amount is deliberately NOT in the numbers; "Gesamt" dated "seit {firstCoveredDate}"). Sign-honest framing is the pure `proofLine()` in `src/fleet.ts`: DV fleets read revenue (`Erlös mit VoltPilot` = -actual), everyone else costs - flipping to revenue framing on its own when the period is net-negative (never confusing "negative Stromkosten"). Single-site customers AND the fleet drill-down render the same hero site-scoped (the tenant-wide response is filtered client-side); the single-site Übersicht's old savings/Ø-Preis KPI row is gone (Ø price moved into the count strip). Site cards show the measured `Heute +X €` teaser via `savedOnDay` (it is history, so it shows even while the live snapshot is stale); structurally non-computable sites (`missing_channels`/`no_prices`) say "Für diesen Standort nicht berechenbar" while `no_data` keeps the calmer onboarding copy.
- **Dev seed `db/dev/V20260706030000`:** 32 days of deterministic PT15M DE-LU prices (ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING - collector rows win) + 30 days of 15m/1h/1d rollups for Dachau + Lindenberg ONLY. Berlin deliberately gets none: the api test suite runs the dev migrations too, and the history/purge tests pin exact Berlin rollup contents. Related discipline: `seedHistoryDay`'s price insert uses `ON CONFLICT ... DO UPDATE` because the seed's rolling 32-day price window can cover the test's fixed 2026-06-15 date - a test with hand-computed expectations must OWN its price slots.
- **Tests:** `PortalApiTest.earningsComputesRealizedSavingsPerSiteWithHonestDegradation` (hand-computed two-zone seed, PT15M-beats-PT60M decoy, PT60M fallback hour, unpriced gap slot, the algebraic identity, all three degradation reasons, per-site dailySaved, range=all + default-month, RLS, 400s); portal `src/fleet.test.ts` (proofLine framing, range wording, fine print, teaser, daily helpers).
- **The Anlagen-scharfe twin `GET /api/v1/sites/{siteId}/earnings` (`SiteEarningsController`, Historie concept F1 / measure P3).** The tenant-wide endpoint answers a PORTFOLIO question and pays for it (measured 3-7,5 s: five aggregates over every site, plus strip and forward market value); the Erlöse world of ONE Anlage asks a smaller question and gets three site-scoped queries over the SAME SQL. `EarningsRepository` grew an optional site filter (`aggregateForSite`/`arbitrageSplitForSite`/`bucketedForSite`/`tarifPricedForSite` - ONE query text, a `siteFilter(site)` fragment plus the matching bind), so there is still exactly ONE price truth and ONE money model.
  - **Three sums were EXPOSED, not invented:** `stromkostenEur` (the metered import at `SlotEconomics.importPriceCtSql` - literally the import term of `actualEur`), `bezogenKwh` (so the Ø Bezugspreis is a division of two SHOWN sums, `bezugspreisCtKwh`) and `marktpraemieEur` (the `ACTUAL_PREMIUM_EUR` term already contained in `einspeiseErloesEur`). `BucketPoint` carries `stromkostenEur` too, so the money chart can stack the three parts per bucket. **Two identities hold by construction and are asserted exactly:** `nettoErgebnisEur = einspeise + eigenverbrauchsWert − stromkosten` and `stromkosten − einspeise = actualEur`.
  - **Ranges are the HISTORIE vocabulary** (`day|week|month|year|all`) - unlike the fleet endpoint, `week` is offered, because the Erlöse world's time bar always had it; the series bucket follows the period (P6: hour / day / day / month). `savedEur` stays an ATTRIBUTION inside the result and is never a sibling summand. RLS fences it like every site route (foreign site 404, admins via the `X-Tenant-Id` switcher).
  - **Tests:** `PortalApiTest.siteEarningsAnswerOneAnlageWithItsReconcilingComposition` (hand-computed two current-month CH slots on a `fest` 30-ct site: both identities, the Ø Bezugspreis, the day-bucketed series, `week` accepted / an unknown range 400, honest nulls + `reason` for a site without data, RLS 404); portal `src/erloesKomposition.test.ts` + `src/pages/HistorieWelten.test.tsx`.

## Negative-price curtailment + Marktprämie (fleet overview Phase 3)

Captain decisions #2/#3 (2026-07-06): the plan MAY curtail PV at negative prices, and a configured Marktprämie enters the realized-earnings math. One api migration **`V20260706040000`** carries both columns (`schedule.curtail_kw`, `site.marktpraemie_ct_kwh`).

- **Curtailment in the MILP:** `curtail_t in [0, pv_t]` joins the grid balance (`grid = load - pv + curtail + charge - discharge`), so a full battery at negative prices no longer FORCES a paid export. Deliberately NO price condition in the model - under symmetric spot pricing the economics curtail exactly when the price is negative (full curtailment then, since paid import beats own PV) and never at positive prices; details incl. the two epsilon tie-breaks in the "Optimization engine" section and `solver.py`'s docstring. Persisted per slot (`curtail_kw`), published as the OPTIONAL per-slot **`pv_limit_kw`** (= `pv - curtail`, an inverter feed-in CAP so a forecast overshoot is capped too; omitted when not curtailing, keeping non-curtailing payloads byte-identical) - an ADDITIVE schedule-contract extension, `schema_version` stays 1.0 (see Contracts).
- **Edge execution (dev edge + sim):** the SunSpec sim gains writable register 42 (`pv_limit`, uint16 0.01 kW, **0xFFFF sentinel = no limit**, power-on default; the model takes `min(diurnal PV, limit)` - a cap can only ever reduce). The Node-RED flow's slot selector passes `pv_limit_kw` through, the Guards clamp it to `>= 0` (SAFETY: never a negative limit, never a command to produce, battery caps untouched) and write FC6 addr 42 alongside the battery setpoint; **absent/invalid limit and the Default-Watchdog fallback write the 0xFFFF sentinel**, so a stale curtailment never outlives its schedule. The customer **edge-app ignores the field** (Go `plan.Parse` fixed struct) - real curtailment execution there is future work; its guards/fallback behavior is unchanged.
- **Marktprämie in earnings: SUPERSEDED (captain domain fix 2026-07-07).** The Phase-3 `site.marktpraemie_ct_kwh` (a manually entered FIXED ct/kWh premium) had the wrong semantics - fixed is the plant's ANZULEGENDER WERT, the premium is the dynamic monthly difference to the Monatsmarktwert Solar. See the section "Dynamic Marktprämie (anzulegender Wert x Monatsmarktwert Solar)" below; the old column is deprecated (kept one release, no reader/writer, `openapi.yaml` marks it), values were NOT migrated (different quantity). The both-sides crediting + simplified §51 suspension rule carried over unchanged.
- **Portal + tests for the premium moved with the dynamic model** - see "Dynamic Marktprämie" below. The curtailment tests (optimizer + contract) in the Optimization-engine bullet are unaffected.

## Per-site grid-charging switch (`site.netzladen_erlaubt`, EEG compliance)

Captain decisions 2026-07-07 (binding: firstmate `vp-grid-charging-modes`): EEG-funded plants must never charge their battery from the grid (Ausschliesslichkeitsprinzip), so grid arbitrage is an ADMIN-gated opt-in per site.
One api migration **`V20260707000000`** adds `site.netzladen_erlaubt BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE` - every existing plant stays compliant until explicitly switched.

- **One optimizer, ONE conditional constraint - PV-bus Bilanzierung since FK3 (captain decisions: switch 2026-07-07, semantics 2026-07-16, audit `vp-solver-xlsx-f2` §4.6).** `inputs.py` reads the flag per site; when FALSE, `solver.py` adds (in the primary AND the infeasible-§14a fallback build) `charge_t <= max(pv_t, 0) - curtail_t`: the battery may charge up to the full PV actually PRODUCED **while the house imports its load in parallel** (DC-hybrid physics, "wie meine Excel" - the pre-FK3 Zähler interpretation `charge <= max(pv − load, 0)` plus an import-ban-while-charging was strictly tighter and forbade charging on cloudy days the reference spec allows). Subtracting `curtail` is load-bearing: it replaces the removed import ban as the CURTAILMENT-loophole closure - a fully curtailed slot cannot charge, and via the grid balance a charging slot's import never exceeds the load, so grid energy can never enter the battery (Graustrom excluded, the pricing layer's premium-eligibility argument preserved). Curtailment itself stays available to EEG sites (it limits feed-in, not charge availability). Merchant mode (`true`) builds the byte-identical pre-switch model - the untouched arbitrage tests + `test_eeg_constraint_exists_only_in_eeg_mode_and_in_both_builds` pin the regression. `OptimizationInput.netzladen_erlaubt` is REQUIRED (no default) so a forgotten wire-up can never silently put an EEG plant into arbitrage.
- **Editing: the SITE OWNER may switch it too (captain REVISION of decision 3, 2026-07-07 midday, screenshot feedback - the original decision was ADMIN-ONLY).** `CreateSiteRequest`/`UpdateSiteRequest` carry a nullable `netzladenErlaubt` that any caller may set on the sites they reach - RLS already scopes that to the caller's own tenant, so no extra role gate remains (`SiteController.requireAdminForNetzladen` is REMOVED); admins keep working via the tenant switcher and the admin create path. Null on update keeps the stored value (`COALESCE` in `SiteRepository.update`), so a form that omits the field never flips the flag; the DB default stays FALSE. On `SiteDto`/`OverviewSiteDto` + `openapi.yaml`. Proven by `AdminApiTest.netzladenSwitchIsEditableByTheSiteOwnerAndAdmins` (customer sets on create + flips both ways on update of their OWN site; omitted field = default FALSE on create / stored value on update; a FOREIGN tenant's site stays RLS-404 and provably unchanged; admin create + tenant-switcher flip keep working; overview echoes the flag).
- **Portal visibility (captain decision 4 + the decision-3 revision):** the shared `NetzladenBadge` component ("Nur Solarladen (EEG)" green / "Netzladen aktiv" cyan; wording in `fleet.ts netzladenBadge`) on the fleet site card + the Standort detail drawer; the "Netzladen des Speichers" select renders for EVERYONE in `SiteEditForm`/`CreateSiteDrawer` (the former `isAdmin`/`admin` gating props are gone) with the Ausschliesslichkeitsprinzip warning copy carrying the responsibility ("Nur aktivieren, wenn Ihre Anlage keine EEG-Vergütung bezieht"); the old read-only "Änderung nur durch den Betreiber (Portal-Admin)" note is retired. The **Fahrplan chart** colors grid-charge slots cyan (`--vp-chart-gridcharge`), DERIVED from slot data - PV-AWARE since the FK3 display follow-up (2026-07-16): a charging slot is `netzladen` only when the charge EXCEEDS the slot's available PV (`batteryKw > pvKw - curtailKw + 0.1 kW` deadband while importing; `src/schedule.ts chargeKind`, per-slot PV via the additive `ScheduleSlotDto.pvKw` = the persisted `schedule.pv_kw`), so an EEG cloudy-day slot that charges solar WHILE the house imports its load stays green and "no türkis = the visible EEG proof" holds again under PV-bus semantics; slots without a PV value (pre-pvKw rows) fall back to the old charging-while-importing rule. The same rule drives the legend gating (`hasGridCharge`), the mini-preview bars/sentence and the admin diagnostics' server twin `SlotEconomics.decisionLabel` (kept in sync BY HAND - change both together). Deliberately NO list column (captain chose badge+chart only).
- **Dev seed** `db/dev/V20260707000100` flips Solarpark Dachau to erlaubt (UPDATE = inherently existence-guarded no-op when absent), so the demo fleet shows both modes.
- **Tests:** optimizer `test_solver.py` EEG section (never grid-charges under extreme spread with no PV, the FK3 cloudy-day proof - load > pv all day charges up to produced PV while the house imports, never beyond pv; the Graustrom loophole test - a fully-curtailed slot cannot charge and a charging slot's import never exceeds the load, incl. the merchant contrast; §14a interplay + EEG-clean fallback; structural both-builds proof); api `AdminApiTest.netzladenSwitchIsAdminOnlyEnforcedServerSide`; portal `schedule.test.ts` (chargeKind/hasGridCharge) + `fleet.test.ts` (badge wording). NOTE the Fahrplan chart's cyan "Laden aus dem Netz" derivation is pv-aware (see the portal-visibility bullet): an EEG cloudy-day slot (solar charge while the house imports) stays green; `schedule.test.ts` + `SlotEconomicsTest.decisionLabelIsPvAwareSinceFk3` pin it on both sides.
- **Out of scope (deliberate):** cycle/wear limits, grid fees/asymmetric import pricing (the earnings-hero arbitrage split is now BUILT - see the next section).

## Arbitrage-Ausweis ("davon Arbitrage-Gewinn" in the earnings hero)

Captain pick 2026-07-07 (review-session decision 5, task vp-arbitrage-ausweis): a grid-charging site's earnings hero shows what the netzladen permission concretely earned.
No schema change - a read-side attribution over the existing rollups.

- **The honest split (storage-mix model, `EarningsRepository.arbitrageSplit`):** a deterministic one-pass walk over a netzladen site's covered 15-min slots in time order, carrying the battery content as two pools (pv-charged vs grid-charged kWh; grid share of a slot's charge = `max(0, charge - max(pv - load, 0))`, charge applied before discharge, discharge draws proportionally from the mix). `arbitrageEur` = grid-attributed discharge revenue minus grid-charge purchase cost; `pvShiftEur` is computed as the REMAINDER `saved - arbitrage`, so the reconciliation `arbitrage + pvShift == saved` holds EXACTLY by construction. Documented assumptions (method Javadoc): pre-window battery content counts as solar (conservative - never inflates arbitrage), round-trip losses debit both pools proportionally, sub-slot interleaving is invisible (slot granularity, same as the saved total), the Marktprämie delta stays entirely on the PV side (grid-charged energy is never premium-eligible), and the flag is read at query time (no historical mode tracking).
- **Null, never a fake zero:** both fields are null for `netzladen_erlaubt = false` sites AND for windows without grid-charged energy (the repo only returns sites with `gridChargedKwh > 0`). Fleet totals: `arbitrageEur` sums the split sites; `pvShiftEur = savedEur - arbitrageEur` over the WHOLE fleet (no-split sites cannot grid-charge, so their entire saved is PV-shift) - fleet-level reconciliation holds too. On `EarningsSiteDto`/`EarningsTotalsDto` + `openapi.yaml`.
- **Portal:** one calm extra line under the hero proof ("davon durch Netzladen verdient: +X €"; a losing period says "Netzladen hat in diesem Zeitraum X € gekostet" - sign-honest, wording in `fleet.ts arbitrageLine`), and the fine print gains the one-sentence attribution ("Der Netzladen-Anteil ist der Verkaufserlös der aus dem Netz geladenen Energie abzüglich ihrer Einkaufskosten.") only while the line shows. Renders for fleet totals AND the site-scoped hero (`HeroMoney.arbitrageEur`); per-site card teaser unchanged. CSS `.vp-fleet-hero-arb`.
- **Demo-data caveat:** the dev seed's Dachau charges 40 kW against a ~67 kW midday PV surplus, so it never grid-charges and the demo hero shows NO arbitrage line (the line needs real grid-charge slots; seed them manually when demoing).
- **Tests:** `PortalApiTest.earningsSplitArbitrageFromPvShiftForGridChargingSites` (hand-computed multi-slot day: pre-window discharge -> solar, cheap-night grid charge, noon PV charge, evening 50/50 mix draw; exact reconciliation per site + fleet; EEG twin with identical measurements gets NO split; solar-only merchant gets NO split; RLS); portal `fleet.test.ts` (`arbitrageLine` wording incl. negative + negative-zero, fine-print sentence gating).

## Dynamic Marktprämie (anzulegender Wert x Monatsmarktwert Solar) + Marktwert-Benchmark

Captain domain fix 2026-07-07 (task vp-marktpraemie-dynamisch): the EEG premium is NOT a fixed ct/kWh - fixed is the plant's **anzulegender Wert** (its EEG reference rate); the premium per exported kWh in month M is `max(0, anzulegender_wert - monatsmarktwert_solar(M))`, suspended in negative-price slots (the simplified §51 rule kept), credited on BOTH earnings sides exactly like before.

- **Field:** `site.anzulegender_wert_ct_kwh` (api migration **`V20260707020000`**, nullable NUMERIC >= 0). The old `site.marktpraemie_ct_kwh` is DEPRECATED, not migrated (a fixed premium and a reference rate are different quantities; the column shipped one day earlier, so meaningful prod values are unlikely but unverifiable from the sandbox - hence keep-one-release, echoed read-only on `SiteDto`, dropped from Create/Update requests, `deprecated: true` in `openapi.yaml`; a follow-up migration removes it). Forms (`CreateSiteDrawer`, `AnlageTechnik` `SiteEditForm`) now say "Anzulegender Wert (ct/kWh)" with the help "Steht in Ihrem EEG-Zuschlag bzw. Direktvermarktungsvertrag", DV-only as before.
- **Monatsmarktwert-Solar feed (`services/market-data`):** new `market_value.py` (port `MarketValueSource` + `MonthlyMarketValue` + the provisional calc) and `netztransparenz.py` - a **KEYLESS** adapter against the endpoint feeding netztransparenz.de's own Marktwertübersicht chart (`POST /DesktopModules/LotesCharts/Services/HighchartService.asmx/GetMarketpremiumData`, plain JSON, verified live 2026-07-07, data back to 2012; the OFFICIAL WebAPI needs a registered OAuth2 client, so it is NOT keyless - documented upgrade path if the chart endpoint ever moves; energy-charts has no market-value endpoint). The `MW Solar` column is located BY NAME, never position. Table **`monthly_market_value`** (service migration `V20260707001000`; api `V20260707020000` is the idempotent runtime twin + app-role SELECT grant; mirrors in `infra/local/timescale/05-market-value.sql` + `infra/prod/timescale/00-collector-tables.sql`): market-wide, NO tenant/RLS, deliberately NOT a hypertable (twelve rows/technology/year); PK `(technology, month)`, `month` = first day of the GERMAN calendar month, `provisional` flag. The `serve --persist` loop refreshes it every cycle (previous + current year), and `market-values [--persist]` is the one-shot CLI; upsert rule: published always overwrites, provisional NEVER overwrites published.
- **Provisional value (months the TSOs have not published yet - always the running month, usually also the previous one until ~the 10th working day):** computed from the stored DE-LU day-ahead prices as a **solar-shape-weighted average** - weights = `max(0, sin(solar elevation))` over the geographic centre of Germany (51.16N 10.45E, NOAA-style declination + hour angle, `solar_shape_weight`). Documented approximation: reproduces the diurnal/seasonal envelope but ignores clouds, so it sits slightly closer to the plain average than the official generation-weighted number - acceptable for a value the API labels `provisional` and the portal labels "vorläufig". No stored prices for a month => NO row => no premium (never invented).
- **Earnings (`EarningsRepository`):** `MARKET_VALUE_JOIN` maps each rollup bucket's Europe/Berlin month to `monthly_market_value` ('solar'); `PREMIUM_RATE_CT = GREATEST(anzulegender_wert - mv.value, 0)`; eligibility additionally requires a market-value row. NULL anzulegender Wert OR missing month => exactly the pure-spot numbers (regression-pinned). The **benchmark KPI** rides the same aggregate: `realizedExportCtKwh` (export-weighted spot the feed-in fetched, EUR/MWh / 10), `marketValueSolarCtKwh` (Monatsmarktwert weighted with the SAME exports - like-for-like across months), `marketValueProvisional` (bool_or over contributing months) - null without exports/coverage, never fake zeros; separate denominators so a missing month degrades the benchmark, not the realized number.
- **Portal:** `premiumIncluded()` keys on `anzulegenderWertCtKwh`; `premiumDetail()` (exactly ONE rendered DV site with value + benchmark, else generic wording) feeds the detailed fine print "Inkl. Marktprämie: anzulegender Wert X ct − Monatsmarktwert Solar Y ct (vorläufig), entfällt bei negativen Preisen"; `marktwertBenchmark()` renders the calm KPI line "Sie haben X,X ct/kWh für Ihren eingespeisten Strom erzielt − Monatsdurchschnitt Solar: Y,Y ct" in the site-scoped `EarningsHero` (Anlagen-Seite; the fleet hero keeps generic wording - mixed reference rates never average).
- **Dev seed** `db/dev/V20260707020100`: Dachau gets anzulegender Wert 8.11 ct/kWh + four now()-relative MW-Solar months (current one provisional), `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` so collector rows win; tests that pin premium numbers must OWN their months via `ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE` (the seedHistoryDay price rule).
- **Tests:** market-data `test_market_value.py` (VERBATIM live fixtures incl. the unpublished-months state, column-by-name proof, month-key/garbage rejections, provisional calc hand-verified incl. duck-curve/night-immunity/Berlin-month scoping, refresh glue, published-beats-provisional both ways); api `PortalApiTest.earningsUseTheDynamicMonthlyPremiumFromAnzulegenderWertAndMonatsmarktwert` (hand-computed month-boundary seed: two different monthly premia, floored month, no-MW month, negative-slot suspension, NULL regression, both-sides crediting, benchmark KPI values + provisional flag, validation/edit/RLS); portal `fleet.test.ts` (detail fine print, `premiumDetail`, `marktwertBenchmark` wording).

## Money-centric "Meine Anlage" v2 (`GET /api/v1/earnings` money-view fields + `site.tarif_art`/`tarif_param_ct_kwh`)

Captain feature 2026-07-07 (`vp-anlage-money-view`, modelled on the Deye Copilot app): the per-Anlage page leads with the **Gesamtertrag** in euros, period tabs govern the whole page, a tappable 12-month strip, an Ertrag chart. The portal side is in `frontend/portal/AGENTS.md` ("Meine Anlage v2" + "Single-plant merge"); the backend extends the existing PR-93 earnings engine (same files, additive). The tariff model + Nachvollziehbarkeit below (task `vp-anlage-nachvollziehbar`, 2026-07-08) supersede the original fixed `strompreis_ct_kwh`.

- **Tariff model `site.tarif_art` + `site.tarif_param_ct_kwh` (api migration `V20260708010000`, task `vp-anlage-nachvollziehbar`) - REPLACES the fixed `site.strompreis_ct_kwh`.** A single fixed retail price is wrong for the many customers on a DYNAMIC tariff (price changes every quarter hour; only the anzulegender Wert is truly fixed). Cleanest schema = ONE enum + ONE numeric (not two fixed/dynamic numerics): `tarif_art` is `dynamisch | fest | ohne` (default `ohne`), `tarif_param_ct_kwh` is its parameter - the fixed retail price for `fest`, the OPTIONAL spot-price Aufschlag (grid fees/levies/margin) for `dynamisch`, unused for `ohne`. The migration copies the old `strompreis_ct_kwh` into `fest` + that value (null -> `ohne`, the least-surprising default that reproduces the kWh-only behaviour) and DROPS the column (unlike the deprecated `marktpraemie_ct_kwh`, strompreis IS faithfully migratable, so it is migrated + dropped, no dead column). Pure additive ALTER (V2 RLS/grants cover it); on `SiteDto` + `CreateSiteRequest`/`UpdateSiteRequest` (validated; `tarif_art` overwrites like `plant_kind`, `ohne` clears the param via `tarifParamOrNull()`) + `AdminSiteRepository`; edited in the portal via the shared `components/TariffFields.tsx` on the SETTINGS/Technik subpage ONLY (captain decision 5 - the money view shows only results), NOT the money hero. Dev seed `db/dev/V20260708010100` sets Hof Lindenberg to `dynamisch` + 18 ct/kWh Aufschlag.
- **`EarningsController`/`EarningsRepository` per-site fields** (all null when nothing computable, same `reason`): `einspeiseErloesEur` (metered export x spot + Marktprämie - the feed-in revenue), `selbstverbrauchKwh` (= `greatest(load - grid_import, 0)`), **`eigenverbrauchsWertEur`** - now computed SLOT-BY-SLOT in the repository per the site's tariff (`EIGENVERBRAUCHS_WERT_EUR` SQL): `dynamisch` = `sum(selbstverbrauch_kwh * (price_eur_mwh/1000 + Aufschlag/100))` so each self-consumed kWh is valued at ITS OWN 15-min Börsenpreis + the fixed Aufschlag (the whole point - NOT a single flat price; the spot part can go negative in a negative-price slot, which is correct - the §51 suspension is a feed-in rule, not a self-consumption one); `fest` = `sum(selbstverbrauch_kwh * price/100)`; `ohne` = NULL (never a fabricated euro). Because it needs the per-slot spot price it MUST live in SQL, not the controller - the old `selbstverbrauchKwh x strompreis` controller helper is gone. `gesamtertragEur` = `einspeiseErloesEur + (eigenverbrauchsWertEur ?? 0)`; `eingespeistKwh`/`batterieBewegtKwh` (energy sums), `tarifArt`/`tarifParamCtKwh` (echo, so the portal can render the provenance sentence), plus `series` + `monthlyStrip` (both carry the tariff-priced Gesamtertrag per bucket). `BucketPoint` now carries `eigenverbrauchsWertEur` per bucket (SQL-computed) instead of raw `selbstverbrauchKwh`.
- **Nachvollziehbarkeit (captain decision 3):** each euro figure in the money hero carries an expandable `InfoTip` whose ONE German sentence names the real quantities + prices - pure builders in `src/anlage.ts` (`einspeiseProvenance`/`eigenverbrauchProvenance`/`gesamtertragProvenance`/`savedProvenance`, unit-tested in `anlage.test.ts`): e.g. "Selbst verbrauchte 97,3 kWh × dynamischer Börsenpreis + 18,0 ct/kWh Aufschlag (im Schnitt 27,3 ct/kWh)" - the effective average is derived from `eigenverbrauchsWertEur/selbstverbrauchKwh`, so the copy is real not hardcoded. The Ertrag chart gained a "Was zeigt das?" `ChartSubtitle` (Ertrag = Einspeisung + Wert des Eigenverbrauchs) + the best-bucket moved into a `ChartInsight`. The three energy tiles renamed to "Eingespeist / Selbst genutzt / Über Batterie" + a one-line hint each (`energyTiles()` in `anlage.ts`). Read-only Technik row via `fleet.ts tarifArtLabel`.
- **`EarningsRepository.bucketed(from, to, Bucket)`** buckets Gesamtertrag parts (`einspeiseErloesEur`/`selbstverbrauchKwh`) by Europe/Berlin **HOUR/DAY/MONTH** - one method, called twice: the range series (hour for day, day for month, month for year/all) and the 12-month strip (month buckets over the last year, anchored on today independent of `at`). Bucket start = `date_trunc(<unit>, r.bucket AT TIME ZONE 'Europe/Berlin') AT TIME ZONE 'Europe/Berlin'` (guaranteed correct across DST/month-length, no TimescaleDB month-bucket dependency; the unit is a fixed enum literal, never client input). `GET /earnings?range=&at=` already existed; the portal passes `at` for a strip-tapped past month.
- **Tests:** `PortalApiTest.earningsExposeGesamtertragEnergyAndSeriesForTheMoneyView` (hand-computed two current-month CH slots, a `fest` 30 ct/kWh tariff + an `ohne` twin: einspeise/selbstverbrauch/eigenverbrauchsWert/Gesamtertrag reconciliation `gesamt == einspeise + eigenverbrauchsWert`, energy sums, DAY series + month strip, the `ohne` kWh-only regression, RLS) + **`earningsValueDynamicSelfConsumptionSlotBySlotAtSpotPlusAufschlag`** (three CH slots at 100/200/-40 EUR/MWh with an 18 ct Aufschlag - proves the value follows each slot's spot price and differs from a `fest` twin with identical measurements; the negative slot proves the spot part goes down while the Aufschlag keeps it positive; a previous-month slot the range=month window excludes). Portal `anlage.test.ts` (provenance sentences incl. the dynamic average, energy-tile copy) + `fleet.test.ts` (`tarifArtLabel`). Verified end-to-end in the browser against an isolated stack (1440/768/375): the money hero with real numbers, the `eigenverbrauchProvenance` InfoTip showing "Selbst verbrauchte 97,3 kWh × dynamischer Börsenpreis + 18,0 ct/kWh Aufschlag (im Schnitt 27,3 ct/kWh)" clamped within the 375 viewport, the Tarif-Art form on the Technik gear subpage, and zero horizontal overflow.

## Forward expected Marktwert Solar (`GET /api/v1/earnings` `expectedMarketValueSolarCtKwh`)

Captain decision 2026-07-09 (`vp-expected-marktwert-solar`): a per-Anlage, FORWARD-looking market value - what the market values THIS plant's coming production, weighted by its own PV forecast. The forward companion to the realized/backward `marketValueSolarCtKwh`. Additive, no data-model change.

- **The calc (`EarningsRepository.expectedMarketValue(pvModel, now)`):** `Σ(price(t) × pv_forecast(t)) / Σ(pv_forecast(t))` over the forward slots where BOTH a day-ahead price AND this site's PV forecast exist, then EUR/MWh `/10` -> **ct/kWh** (same unit as `marketValueSolarCtKwh`). Data sources REUSED, not rebuilt: `day_ahead_prices` (same `PRICE_LATERAL` slot match as the realized aggregates, joined per the site's OWN `bidding_zone`, PT15M preferred over PT60M) and the `forecast` hypertable (`kind='pv'`, LATEST `run_at` of the **ACTIVE** PV model only - shadow challengers ignored, same rule as the optimizer's `inputs.py`; the api reads the active model from `voltpilot.forecast.active-pv-model`). The forecast is POWER (`forecast.value_kw`, kW); the constant 15-min slot length cancels in the weighted average, so weighting by power == weighting by energy. Negative-price slots are INCLUDED (they legitimately pull the value down); zero-production night slots contribute 0 to both sums and never distort the weighting.
- **RLS:** `forecast` carries NO RLS (backend-only consumers), so the query JOINs the RLS-scoped `site` table to fence it to the caller's tenant - the same technique the realized aggregates use (no tenant predicate). `now` is `Instant.now()`; the figure is RANGE-INDEPENDENT (always the coming horizon), computed once per request regardless of `range`.
- **DTO:** `EarningsSiteDto.expectedMarketValueSolarCtKwh` + `expectedMarketValueFrom`/`...To` (covered forward-slot bounds) + `expectedMarketValueSlots` (count, so the portal can say "nächste N h"). ALL FOUR null (site absent from the repo map -> controller emits nulls) when there is no forward PV forecast or no forward price coverage (Σ(pv)=0 -> value NULL -> row dropped) - never a fabricated 0. In `docs/contracts/openapi.yaml`.
- **Portal:** one calm line under the money hero, see `frontend/portal/AGENTS.md` ("Forward Erwarteter Marktwert Solar"). Derivation in `src/anlage.ts` `expectedMarketValueLine`.
- **Tests:** `PortalApiTest.earningsExposeTheForwardExpectedMarketValueWeightedByPvForecast` (forward-seeded relative to `now()` so it exercises the real `time >= now()` filter: three CH slots 100/200/-40 EUR/MWh weighted 2/6/1 kW -> `1360/9/10` ct/kWh, a priced zero-PV night slot that widens the horizon but not the value, a shadow-challenger row that must be ignored, a forecast-but-no-price site -> null, range-independence, RLS); portal `anlage.test.ts`.

## Market data (ENTSO-E day-ahead prices)

`services/market-data` is the anti-corruption adapter that feeds day-ahead spot prices into the optimizer (architecture section 11/13: "Marktdaten: ENTSO-E Transparency, hinter Adapter").

- **Port / providers.** `DayAheadPriceSource` (in `source.py`) is the provider-agnostic port; every caller depends on it, never on ENTSO-E. `EntsoeDayAheadPriceSource` is the first implementation; a commercial provider is a drop-in replacement. `ResilientPriceSource` decorates any source with retry (exponential backoff), a circuit breaker, and a **last-good cache**, so the optimizer always gets a usable series even when ENTSO-E is down.
- **Internal representation.** `PriceSeries` / `PricePoint` (EUR/MWh, UTC slot bounds, `PT15M`/`PT60M`). No vendor vocabulary crosses this boundary.
- **Zone -> EIC mapping** lives only in `zones.py`: `DE-LU -> 10Y1001A1001A82H`, `AT -> 10YAT-APG------L`, `CH -> 10YCH-SWISSGRIDZ`. DE-LU is implemented end to end; adding AT/CH is a one-line edit there.
- **Storage.** Prices are timeseries -> the `day_ahead_prices` **hypertable**, keyed by `(bidding_zone, resolution, ts)`. Prices are market-wide **per bidding zone, not per tenant**, so there is deliberately **no `tenant_id`** (and no RLS) on this table, unlike `telemetry`. Schema is owned by the forward-only migration `services/market-data/db/migration/V20260701001200__day_ahead_prices_hypertable.sql` (Flyway/Liquibase-compatible; **date-based version chosen so it does not collide with the api service's future `V1, V2, ...` baseline** - coordinate future market-data migrations to stay in this `V2026...` scope). The local dev stack mirrors it via `infra/local/timescale/02-day-ahead-prices.sql` (additive; the existing `01-init.sql` is untouched).
- **Token.** `ENTSOE_SECURITY_TOKEN` is a **captain-provided secret** (blank in `.env.example`); obtain via ENTSO-E Transparency registration + a "Restful API access" email (see `services/market-data/README.md`). It is **not** needed for tests/CI - parsing, mapping and resilience run entirely off recorded fixtures in `tests/fixtures/`. A live token is needed only for a real end-to-end fetch; that end-to-end verification is still open.
- **Fetch cadence.** ENTSO-E publishes the next day's prices ~12:45 market time; run `python -m voltpilot_market_data fetch --zone DE-LU --persist` daily after that (cron `0 13 * * *`, or a future K8s CronJob from the service `Dockerfile`). The CLI `--zone` defaults to the `MARKET_DATA_ZONE` env var (fallback `DE-LU`) when omitted, so the container `CMD` is just `fetch --persist` and picks its zone from the environment. Following the backbone-first convention, the service is **not** wired into `docker-compose.yml`.

## Forecast service (`services/forecast`)

Load/PV forecasts for the optimizer (architecture section 12). Pure Python, dependency-free core, offline-first (demo + tests need no DB/network); the ML challengers live behind the optional `ml` extra. Full detail in `services/forecast/README.md`; the shadow-mode model registry, the challengers and the evaluation have their own section below ("Shadow-mode forecasting").

- **Methods.** Load = baseline (`SeasonalPersistenceLoadForecaster`, `ProfileLoadForecaster`; persistence/profile per architecture 12). PV = physical model (`PhysicalPvForecaster`): solar geometry -> clear-sky irradiance -> plane-of-array transposition -> PVWatts-style capacity/derate. Day-ahead price is not forecast (ENTSO-E-given).
- **A forecast slot is a quarter-hour MEAN, and the aggregation lives in PYTHON, not in SQL (prod defect, fixed 2026-07-30, scout `vp-netzbezug-nacht-s3` §3 link 3).** Telemetry arrives at ~5-10 s, so ~90-180 raw samples fall into one 15-min slot. `SeasonalPersistenceLoadForecaster` used to keep the LAST of them per slot (`by_slot[key] = obs.value_kw`), which made a quarter-hour forecast a random draw from within the slot - measured on the captain's Pilsting plant: **1,14 kW mean absolute sampling error, 2,26 kW peak**, before any day-to-day deviation. Since the fix every baseline sees history ONLY through `domain.slot_means(history, slot_minutes)` (`features.bucket_15min` is now a thin alias of it, so challengers and baselines provably share ONE aggregation). Two rules follow: **the two telemetry queries (`forecast_collect._telemetry_history`, optimizer `inputs._load_history`) MUST keep returning RAW samples** - a `time_bucket('15 minutes')` there would hard-code the slot width the `Horizon` owns, would have to be kept in sync twice, and would silently change the ML challengers' training rows; and **a new forecaster must aggregate through `slot_means`, never index raw samples by slot**. This matters because the optimizer plans `Entladung = Lastprognose − PV-Prognose` in an Eigenverbrauch slot and the edge executes that setpoint without load-following, so every kW of forecast error is settled at the grid (≈11 ct/kWh spread between import price and feed-in value). The optimizer's fallback path (`voltpilot_optimization.fallback.persistence_forecast`) REUSES the same forecaster and therefore inherits it - both paths plan the same quantity.
- **Interfaces (swap points).** `LoadForecaster`, `PvForecaster`, `WeatherProvider` (weather anti-corruption layer, default `ClearSkyWeatherProvider`), `ForecastRepository`. `ForecastService` is the façade the optimizer calls. The later XGBoost/LightGBM load model and a real EU-hosted weather API implement these same interfaces - consumers are unaffected (architecture 12.2/12.3).
- **Exposure.** Forecasts are timeseries (architecture section 10) -> TimescaleDB `forecast` hypertable, read via `TimescaleForecastRepository` (`.[db]` extra pulls psycopg); `InMemoryForecastRepository` is the offline default. Horizon is 24-48h in 15-min slots.
- **Migration version coordination.** The `forecast` hypertable ships as Flyway `services/forecast/migrations/V3__forecast_hypertable.sql`. **V1/V2 are reserved** for the core schema (master data, telemetry) that `services/api` will own when it adopts Flyway; forecast deliberately takes **V3** to avoid collision. The shadow-mode `model` column is the date-versioned `V20260702000000__forecast_model_column.sql`; the api's `V20260701040000` applies the same DDL idempotently as the canonical runtime owner (the weather_forecast precedent). `infra/local/timescale/02-forecast.sql` and `infra/prod/timescale/00-collector-tables.sql` mirror the same table shape for the bootstraps - keep all four in sync (the migrations are the source of truth).
- **Run/test.** `(cd services/forecast && python -m venv .venv && . .venv/bin/activate && pip install -e '.[dev,ml]' && pytest)` - the `ml` extra pulls xgboost/numpy for the challenger tests (they skip without it; macOS needs `brew install libomp` for the xgboost wheel, Linux libgomp1); `python -m voltpilot_forecast` prints a 24h demo forecast for the seeded demo site. The **weather collector** and the **forecast collector** are wired into the compose `feeds` profile (see below and "Shadow-mode forecasting").

## Keyless data feeds: 15-min day-ahead prices + weather (portal widgets)

Two **KEYLESS** public collectors bring *real* data to the customer dashboard with **no captain secret**, plus tenant-scoped read endpoints and portal widgets. Run both via the compose `feeds` profile:

```bash
docker compose up -d                                                 # backbone + api
docker compose --profile feeds up -d --build market-data weather-collector forecast-collector
```

(The third `feeds` service, **`forecast-collector`**, is the shadow-mode forecasting heartbeat - see its own section.)

- **Day-ahead prices (15-min), energy-charts.info.** `services/market-data` gains a second `DayAheadPriceSource` - `EnergyChartsDayAheadPriceSource` (`energy_charts.py`) against `https://api.energy-charts.info/price?bzn=DE-LU` (Fraunhofer ISE, **no API key**). It is now the **default** source (`--source energy-charts`, env `MARKET_DATA_SOURCE`); ENTSO-E stays available as `--source entsoe` (still needs the token). Resolution is **derived from the data** (900 s spacing -> `PT15M`, 3600 s -> `PT60M`), so it follows the Oct-2025 EPEX shift to a 15-min MTU automatically. It reuses the existing `day_ahead_prices` hypertable + idempotent upsert (`source='energy-charts'`). The new `serve` CLI command refreshes **today+tomorrow on startup then every 6h** (`MARKET_DATA_REFRESH_SECONDS`) and is **coverage-aware** (`refresh.py`, captain-observed 2026-07-17: an unlucky 6-h phase missed the ~12:45 publication by hours and the Fahrplan ended at today's midnight): past `MARKET_DATA_PUBLICATION_HOUR` (Berlin decimal hours, default 12.75) with tomorrow's zone-scoped delivery day not fully covered it polls every `MARKET_DATA_FAST_REFRESH_SECONDS` (default 900) until coverage lands - bounded at local midnight (one loud WARN, never an all-night hammer), and an uncovered pre-threshold cycle caps its sleep AT the threshold so the unlucky phase can't rephase itself; the optimizer needs no change (15-min replans extend the horizon once prices exist). Details/tests: `services/market-data/README.md` + `tests/test_refresh.py`. `fetch` stays the one-shot/cron form. Prices are public per zone (no `tenant_id`, no RLS).
- **Weather forecast, Open-Meteo.** `services/forecast` gains `openmeteo.py` (keyless, EU-hosted `https://api.open-meteo.com/v1/forecast`, hourly `temperature_2m,cloud_cover,shortwave_radiation,direct_radiation,diffuse_radiation`) + `weather_repository.py` + the `weather_collect.py` collector (console script `voltpilot-weather`; `fetch` / `serve`). It reads every site's lat/lon and upserts into a new **`weather_forecast`** hypertable, keyed `(site_id, run_at, time)`. `OpenMeteoWeatherProvider` also adapts a fetched forecast into the PV model's `WeatherProvider` so the physical PV forecaster can consume **measured** irradiance instead of the clear-sky envelope (drop-in, no PV-model change). Weather carries `tenant_id` and is **RLS-scoped like telemetry**; the collector writes as the superuser (bypassing RLS) and stamps each row's tenant from the owning site.
- **Site geo.** `site.latitude`/`site.longitude` (WGS84) tie a site to its weather; the two dev sites are seeded (Berlin 52.52,13.405; Hamburg 53.5511,9.9937). Added by the api migration + the infra mirror; exposed on `SiteDto`.
- **Schema ownership.** The api migration **`V20260701010000__site_geo_and_data_feeds.sql`** (date-versioned so it never collides with api `V1/V2`, forecast `V3`, or market-data `V2026...`) is the canonical owner of the site columns + the read-side of both feed tables: it `CREATE ... IF NOT EXISTS`es `day_ahead_prices` **and** `weather_forecast`, grants the app role `SELECT` on both, and adds the `weather_forecast` RLS policy. This makes the api self-sufficient on a **fresh** DB (Testcontainers/staging) while layering cleanly over the dev bootstrap. Dev mirror: `infra/local/timescale/03-weather.sql` (site cols + `weather_forecast`, **no** RLS/grant - the `voltpilot_app` role doesn't exist at init time; the api migration adds them, exactly like telemetry).
- **API + portal.** `GET /api/v1/sites/{siteId}/prices` (zone series; site resolved via RLS -> 404 if foreign) and `GET /api/v1/sites/{siteId}/weather` (latest run; RLS-scoped) - both in `SiteController`, `openapi.yaml` updated. The portal adds a **day-ahead price bar chart** (15-min bars, colour-graded, today/tomorrow divider) and a **weather panel** (temperature/cloud/GHI) built on the design system + ECharts (`PriceChart.tsx`, `WeatherChart.tsx`, wired into `App.tsx`).
- **Marktpreise historical ranges.** The Marktpreise page (`pages/DataPages.tsx` `MarktpreisePage`) mirrors the **Historie** Tag/Woche/Monat/Jahr period pattern (shared helpers extracted to `src/periodNav.ts`, reused by both pages; same `.vp-seg`/`.vp-period-nav` markup). New endpoint `GET /api/v1/sites/{siteId}/price-history?range=day|week|month|year&at=<date>` (`SiteController.priceHistory` -> `PriceRepository.aggregate`/`summarize`, `PriceHistoryDto`) aggregates `day_ahead_prices` **in SQL via `time_bucket`** (15-min day / hourly week / **Europe/Berlin-daily** month+year - reuses `HistoryRange.window()`; a year is ~365 rows, not ~35k slots). Each bucket carries avg/min/max (the chart's band); the response also returns range summary stats (avg/min/max, cheapest & most-expensive slot ts, slot count, actual data coverage). The **day range on today extends `to` into tomorrow** so the forward-looking day-ahead curve stays visible (the page's core value, kept). `PriceHistoryChart.tsx` renders colour-graded bars with the "Morgen" divider for the day, and an average line + min/max band for week/month/year. Prices are market-wide per zone (no tenant), site-scoped via RLS (foreign site 404). **Data-reality:** the collector only fetches today+tomorrow (no backfill), so historical depth grows one day per collector day - the portal shows a designed empty state and a partial-coverage note ("erst Preise ab dem …") instead of a blank year. KPI values use a **regular** space before `ct/kWh` (not `NBSP`) so the unit wraps under the number in a narrow KPI card instead of being clipped at the tablet 3-across width (a real fix found in browser testing).
- **Tests (the assurance).** `services/market-data/tests/test_energy_charts.py` (parse/resolution-inference/window-clip/rejections + `fetch_and_store` into the in-memory repo, all offline via `FakeHttpClient`); `services/forecast/tests/test_openmeteo.py` (parse + PV-provider adaptation + collector store, offline); the api `PortalApiTest` gains `pricesEndpointReturnsZoneSeries` + `weatherIsTenantScoped` (real TimescaleDB+Keycloak: seed a row as superuser, assert the endpoint + tenant isolation), plus `priceHistoryAggregatesByRangeWithSummaryAndBandStats` + `priceHistoryDayExtendsIntoTomorrowForForwardLookingView` for the ranged endpoint (deterministic **far-past 2023 seed** so other tests' `now()` DE-LU rows never leak into the windows; note Berlin-day buckets serialize at the UTC day-start, so match aggregated days by value, not a `ts` prefix). Portal `npm run build` typechecks the new components. All keyless: no network in tests, no secret anywhere.

## Optimization engine (`services/optimization`)

The product's heart: a deterministic battery-dispatch MILP (Pyomo + HiGHS) in MPC style - rolling **24h horizon, 15-min slots**, re-planned **every 15 min** per site with a battery asset. **Predict-then-optimize** (captain's long-term direction is ML forecasting): forecasting stays a separate, swappable layer; the optimizer only consumes slot-aligned price/forecast series, so learned models later replace the baselines without touching it. Full detail in `services/optimization/README.md`.

- **Inputs per site** (`inputs.py`): battery params from `asset` (capacity/max charge/max discharge existed since V1; **`roundtrip_efficiency_pct`** added by api migration **`V20260701020000`**, dev batteries seeded at 92%), day-ahead prices from `day_ahead_prices` (PT60M rows are expanded to quarter hours; horizon truncates to priced coverage, < 4h skips the site), load/PV forecasts from the `forecast` hypertable - **reading ONLY the ACTIVE model's rows** (`VOLTPILOT_ACTIVE_LOAD_MODEL`/`VOLTPILOT_ACTIVE_PV_MODEL`, defaults = the baselines; see "Shadow-mode forecasting") and **falling back to the persistence baseline over the last 3 days of telemetry when no stored run covers the horizon**, REUSING `voltpilot_forecast` (`fallback.py`; sibling path dep `pip install -e ../forecast`, the Docker build context is therefore the REPO ROOT), latest `soc_pct` + observed `grid_limit_kw` from telemetry - **each behind a FRESHNESS window (F4/P4 fix, optimizer-redesign Stage 1):** a reading older than its window (`OPTIMIZER_GRID_LIMIT_MAX_AGE_MINUTES` default 60 / `OPTIMIZER_SOC_MAX_AGE_MINUTES` default 120; `config.py`, garbage env fails loudly) is IGNORED with a logged warning - a stale §14a reading means "no active limit" (a dimming event is temporary; before this, ONE old reading capped every future plan forever and could force 67% PV curtailment at positive prices), a stale SoC falls back to the 50% default, never yesterday's value. The §14a EXPORT-cap semantics (observed import envelope mirrored onto export - critique F4 part 2, question D5) are DELIBERATELY unchanged: regulatory-adjacent, awaits the captain. **The PV input is night-floored (`fallback.py` `night_floor_pv`, applied in `inputs.py` `_night_floor_pv_input` to the FINAL pv series regardless of source):** the persistence fallback reuses the LOAD forecaster and has NO night-zero knowledge, so on short/reset history it smeared the last daytime PV value across every night slot - phantom night "Solarstrom" that mislabelled a real night grid-charge AND satisfied the EEG `solar_only_charge` constraint (the "Batterie lädt Solarstrom at night" bug). `night_floor_pv` zeros PV wherever `solar_position(site lat/lon, slot).is_daytime` is false (reusing `services/forecast` `solar.py`); a coordinate-less site is left unclamped with a WARN (the physical model is already 0 at night, so the floor is a no-op there - defensive only). `BatterySite`/`load_battery_sites` now carry `site.latitude`/`longitude`. Tests: `tests/test_fallback.py` (short-history phantom PV floored, load untouched, no-coords, and a highspy-gated e2e proving a floored night charge persists a positive `grid_kw` + closes the EEG loophole). **Since Stage 3 (P11), `load_battery_sites` also reads `site.backup_reserve_soc_pct`** (-> `BatteryParams.backup_reserve_pct`, the hard SoC floor - see the MILP bullet). **Since Stage 2 (P1), it also reads the pricing master data** (`site.plant_kind`/`tarif_art`/`tarif_param_ct_kwh`/`anzulegender_wert_ct_kwh` + a LEFT JOIN on the site's `pv` asset for `commissioned_on`/`pv_capacity_kwp` → `BatterySite.tariff`, a `pricing.SiteTariff`), and `gather_inputs` loads `monthly_market_value` rows for premium-eligible DV sites - see the MILP bullet.
- **MILP** (`solver.py`): **market-revenue objective (P1, optimizer-redesign Stage 2 - the core)**: minimize `sum((import_price_t * import_t − export_value_t * export_t) * dt)` **plus PRICED battery wear** (P2, Stage 1): `c_wear/2` per AC-side kWh of charge AND discharge, where `c_wear` = the asset's wear cost per kWh cycled (`asset.wear_cost_ct_per_kwh`, nullable, api migration `V20260710000000`; NULL → env `OPTIMIZER_WEAR_COST_CT_PER_KWH`, default 4.0 ct ≈ 250 €/kWh replacement ÷ 6,000 LFP cycles - derivation in `config.py`). A cycle now needs `eta²·p_discharge − p_charge > ~38 EUR/MWh` at the default (kills the pre-P2 ~3-cycles/day micro-cycling); spent wear is persisted per slot as `schedule.wear_cost_eur` (NOT folded into `cost_eur` - honest net savings = baseline − cost − wear, consumers read it in a later stage). **Asymmetric pricing (`pricing.py`, replaces the symmetric bare-spot F1 bug):** `import_price_t` per `site.tarif_art` (`dynamisch` = spot + Aufschlag, `fest` = flat retail, `ohne` = spot) - **since the structured Bezugspreis (Stufe 1, report vp-nacht-bezug-e7 §3.1)** a maintained **`site_supply_price`** row (api migration `V20260729000000`, one row per site, RLS like `site`: Netzentgelt-AP/Stromsteuer/Konzession/Umlagen-as-ONE-sum/Vertriebsaufschlag ct netto + `ust_pct`) switches `dynamisch`/`ohne` to `(spot + Σ Komponenten netto) × (1 + USt)` (USt covers the SPOT share too), REPLACING the Sammelaufschlag; `fest` stays all-in and IGNORES a maintained row (WARN "fest gewinnt, nichts doppelt"); `dynamisch`+NULL-Aufschlag now WARNs loudly (the S1 night-discharge symptom fix) instead of silently pricing bare spot. **Rollout rule (golden-pinned): NO row + flag off = the exact legacy model, byte-identically** - `tests/test_golden_cooptimizer.py::test_supply_price_rollout_is_byte_identical_without_a_maintained_row` reproduces every golden fixture's committed import series through `import_prices`; a degenerate all-NULL-components row counts as no row. The researched household default set (`DEFAULT_SUPPLY_COMPONENTS`, 15.686 ct netto + 19% USt, report Teil 2) stands in for the no-data cases behind `OPTIMIZER_DEFAULT_SUPPLY_COMPONENTS` (default ON since captain decision 2026-07-29 - a site without a maintained price sheet rechnet mit den Default-Komponenten statt nacktem Spot, opt out with env `false` = the Notbremse; passed to BOTH the optimization and api services in both composes) and never overrides operator data (maintained Aufschlag/row/fest). The api mirror is `SlotEconomics.SupplyPrice` + the 4-arg constructor flag (fed by `OptimizerDiagnosticsRepository`'s `site_supply_price` LEFT JOIN + `OptimizerProperties.defaultSupplyComponents` - vectors shared with `test_pricing.py` in `SlotEconomicsTest`); the portal maintenance UI is BUILT (Stufe 2, PR `fm/vp-bezug-portal-s2`): RLS-fenced `GET/PUT /api/v1/sites/{id}/supply-price` (`SiteSupplyPriceController`/`SiteSupplyPriceRepository`, PATCH semantics = absent field keeps, explicit value writes, explicit null clears a component; `ustPct` NOT NULL so a present-null is kept), the `SupplyPriceFields` Komponentenfelder behind the Tarif-Art select in `TariffFields` (Marktoptimierung Stromtarif-Editor + `AnlageFlow` Feineinstellungen), prefilled with the researched suggestions (`src/supplyPrice.ts` `SUPPLY_PRICE_FIELDS`, editable, visible source+Stand, saved-only-on-confirm - the create flow persists only when the operator TOUCHES a field, never auto-activating the flag), and the admin diagnostics `priceSource` echo (`fest`|`preisblatt`|`sammelaufschlag`|`default-flag`|`spot`, `SlotEconomics.importPriceSource` mirror of `importPriceCtKwh`); the Earnings/History read-side is BUILT (Stufe 3 - see "Realized earnings engine"); `export_value_t` per `site.plant_kind` - Direktvermarktung = spot + dynamic Marktprämie (`max(anzulegender_wert − monthly_market_value, 0)`, suspended at negative spot - byte-for-byte the EarningsRepository rule), eigenverbrauch = feste EEG-Einspeisevergütung from the PV asset's `commissioned_on` + kWp (config-driven schedule in `config.py`: EEG-2023 degression exact, pre-2022 documented annual approximations, tranche-blended, 20-year expiry, §51a-Solarspitzengesetz zero at negative spot for plants ≥ 2025-02-25; wholesale override via `OPTIMIZER_EEG_RATES_JSON`). **EEG remuneration enters the objective ONLY in EEG mode** (`netzladen_erlaubt=false`, where the solar-only-charge bound `charge <= pv − curtail` proves the battery content solar - PV-bus semantics since FK3, see "Per-site grid-charging switch") - a merchant site exports at bare spot (crediting premium on grid-charged energy would be a money pump + Ausschließlichkeitsprinzip violation; inconsistent config logged). **Every missing datum degrades that side to bare spot** (logged), so an unconfigured site is byte-identical to the pre-P1 model - `OptimizationInput.import_price_eur_mwh`/`export_value_eur_mwh` are optional, `None` = spot. Grid balance: import − export = load − pv + **curtail** + charge − discharge; constraints: SoC dynamics with sqrt-split round-trip efficiency, SoC bounds (5-95% platform default), charge/discharge power caps, **curtailment bounded `0 <= curtail_t <= pv_t`** (only ever a REDUCTION of feed-in; economics now key on the EXPORT VALUE, which fixes F6: a pre-2025 feste-Vergütung plant NEVER curtails at negative spot, a DV plant still does), **§14a `grid_limit_kw` as a hard cap on import AND export**, **the FK1 static feed-in cap `site.max_feed_in_kw` as a hard cap on EXPORT ONLY** (nullable > 0 site master data, api migration `V20260716000000`, from the captain's Excel reference spec "Max Einspeisung am Netzpunkt"; on `SiteDto` + create/update requests - null on update KEEPS the stored value, the netzladenErlaubt COALESCE pattern - and edited in the portal's Vergütung & Tarif form + the create flow's Feineinstellungen; in the solver it is deliberately in BOTH builds, i.e. NOT dropped by the infeasible-§14a fallback, because curtailment can always zero the export so it can never cause infeasibility - a 100-kW-PV plant on a 75-kW connection now plans capped export + curtailment/charge; when both caps exist the tighter wins on export, import is NEVER touched by it; `tests/test_excel_spec_day.py` additionally pins the whole Excel reference day: matched config → objective 28.8797 € + the night-discharge/midday-full-charge/evening-discharge structure), and a **terminal ENERGY VALUE instead of the old hard `soc_end >= soc_start` floor (P3, Stage 3)**: the objective credits `V_end * (soc_end - soc_start)` per stored kWh; `OPTIMIZER_TERMINAL_VALUE_CT_PER_KWH` pins a fixed platform value instead. The old floor froze an EEG battery on every low-PV day (critique F3: no surplus -> nothing may charge -> nothing was allowed to discharge, all winter) and forced merchant end-of-horizon buy-backs; now a peak above V_end discharges (incl. energy stored BEFORE the horizon - deliberate, that IS the fix) and a trough/cheap tail below it holds (no dump-to-earn). **`V_end` is a REPLACEMENT cost, not a use value (fix 2026-07-27, scout `vp-fahrplan-idle-n7`; THE ONE derivation is `domain.derive_terminal_value_eur_per_kwh`, shared by the v1 input AND the co-optimizer's per-storage twin - never re-copy it).** It anchors on a low quantile (default 30th pct, `OPTIMIZER_TERMINAL_VALUE_QUANTILE`) of the per-slot REFILL price - `min(import_price, export_value)` where the grid may charge the battery; in EEG mode (only PV may charge) the slot entry splits since S2 (report vp-nacht-bezug-e7 §1.5, 2026-07-29): `export_value` where the slot HAS PV surplus (a refill is on offer, its cost the forgone feed-in), `import_price` where it has none (nothing to refill from - the stored kWh serves the house, worth the avoided Bezug; fixes the winter/bad-weather sell-out where plans dumped the battery at 0.4-6 ct into the last horizon hours while tomorrow's all-in import costs 30+ ct; on surplus-rich horizons the low quantile still lands in the export range, so sunny-day plans are unchanged - the golden suite drifted ZERO slots) - then scales down by the free-PV refill the horizon already offers (surplus the battery could absorb in slots whose export value is <= 0, against its usable band; full coverage -> 0) and is finally held strictly below the BEST in-horizon use value by `TERMINAL_VALUE_MARGIN_EUR_PER_KWH`. **The bug this fixed, worth knowing because the shape recurs:** anchoring on the best USE `max(import, export)` meant a flat retail tariff (`tarif_art='fest'`, import a CONSTANT above every realistic spot peak) produced a ZERO-dispersion series, so any quantile returned retail, `V_end` came out ABOVE the horizon's own peak (28.3 ct vs 20 ct), the discharge gradient was exactly 0.000 and dispatch was structurally impossible - every `fest` plant idled its battery through every evening peak, all year, and wear cancelled symmetrically so Speicherschonung could never help. The margin is sized deliberately BETWEEN the solver gap and the throughput tie-break so it can never override "prefer idle when cycling moves no money"; the guard caps against the horizon's best use value, NOT its worst (the scout's `min(best_use)` one-liner pins `V_end` to a midday trough and re-introduces dump-to-earn into any mediocre tail). Under symmetric pricing (`ohne`) `min == max`, so `dynamisch`/`ohne` plans are unchanged - pinned by `tests/test_flat_tariff_dispatch.py`. **The P11 backup-reserve floor is HARD:** `site.backup_reserve_soc_pct` (nullable, api migration `V20260710010000`; NULL = the 5% technical floor) raises the SoC lower bound via `BatteryParams.soc_floor_kwh` - no price or terminal value ever crosses it (reserve = hard constraint, V_end = soft value; the Deye-Copilot scout documented that product silently draining below its configured min-SoC); a below-reserve start relaxes the floor to the actual start (feasibility; the MPC re-plan ratchets it back up), a reserve above the 95% ceiling pins at the ceiling. Portal/admin UI for the column is follow-up work. **Binaries exclude simultaneous charge+discharge** (at NEGATIVE prices an LP profitably burns energy through the round trip) **AND simultaneous import+export** (load-bearing: whenever export_value > import_price - a real config, e.g. spot-settled DV premium - an LP would farm the difference without physical flow). Two epsilon tie-breaks (~0.004 EUR/MWh scale, never distorting real economics) keep degenerate optima deterministic: prefer NOT curtailing when cost-neutral, prefer an IDLE battery when cycling moves no money. **They only work because `_solve` pins the MIP gap (`MIP_REL_GAP`/`MIP_ABS_GAP` = 1e-9, fix 2026-07-27):** HiGHS defaults to `mip_rel_gap` 1e-4, which on a ~10 EUR objective stops ~1e-3 EUR short of the optimum while a full 15 kW/44-slot cycle carries only ~6.6e-4 EUR of tie-break - so "idle" and "serve the whole evening" were tolerance-equivalent and which one came back was arbitrary, i.e. the committed setpoints could FLIP between two consecutive 15-min re-plans. Tightening the gap changes no economics (inflating the epsilons would); it costs ~10% solve time (golden suite 529 -> 582 ms) and revealed three of the eight golden scenarios were being solved suboptimally at the default. **Never raise the epsilons to "make a tie decide" - raise them only above the gap, or tighten the gap.** Pinned by `tests/test_solver_tolerance.py`. An infeasible §14a cap degrades to a plan without the grid constraint (edge guards + the grid operator enforce it physically anyway) - the fallback build carries curtailment + the EEG solar-only-charge constraint too.
- **Savings headline**: projected EUR vs. the no-battery baseline (same load/PV, battery idle, priced at the SAME asymmetric prices - residual imported at the tariff, surplus fed in at the remuneration), per slot in the DB, summed for the portal ("Heute geplant: X,XX € gespart ggü. ohne Speicher"). Zero on a flat curve by construction. `schedule.price_eur_mwh` stays the SPOT price (portal price curve); `cost_eur`/`baseline_cost_eur` are the asymmetric cashflows.
- **Persistence (ML groundwork)**: every run upserts its full plan into the **`schedule` hypertable** (PK `(site_id, generated_at, time)`; battery/grid power, SoC trajectory, the forecast inputs used, price, cost vs. baseline cost, planned `curtail_kw` - nullable column added by api migration `V20260706040000` -, spent `wear_cost_eur` (nullable, `V20260710000000`) and the run's P3 `terminal_value_eur_per_kwh` (nullable, `V20260716010000` - a RUN-level fact repeated on every slot row, stamped from `effective_terminal_value_eur_per_kwh()` so the portal can show the FK2 banked value; NULL on pre-FK2 runs = display degrades, never a fabricated number), all mirrored in `infra/local/timescale/04-schedule.sql`). Tenant-scoped via RLS exactly like telemetry/weather; the optimizer writes as the trusted backend role and stamps `tenant_id` (weather-collector pattern). Plan-vs-actual is a later join against `telemetry`.
- **Publish**: retained QoS1 to `ems/{t}/{s}/{d}/schedule` per the **frozen** `docs/contracts/mqtt-schedule.schema.json` (see Contracts) for the site's battery `asset.device_id`; sites with an unclaimed battery are persisted but not published. Since Stage 4 (P5) the payload carries the OPTIONAL `grid_charge_allowed` (= `site.netzladen_erlaubt`) so the EDGE enforces EEG solar-only charging against MEASURED values too - closing the F5 gap where a PV forecast overshoot turned a planned "solar" charge into real grid import. Republish cadence (15 min) sits well inside the edge's 20-min staleness window, so the Default-Watchdog only engages on real outages.
- **API + portal**: `GET /api/v1/sites/{siteId}/schedule` (SiteController -> RLS-scoped `ScheduleRepository`, latest run + `savingsEur`; foreign site 404, no plan yet = empty well-formed plan) feeds the portal's **"Fahrplan"** section (`ScheduleChart.tsx`: battery bars over the stepped price line on a shared time axis, planned-SoC trajectory, today/tomorrow divider, savings stats, plus the two dotted FORECAST lines the plan was computed from). **The plan's forecast INPUTS ride along additively:** the slot DTO carries `pvKw` (`schedule.pv_kw`, also the pv-aware chargeKind input) and `loadKw` (`schedule.load_kw`, added 2026-07-29) so the chart can draw PV- und Verbrauchsprognose over the bars - they explain the plan ("warum hält er abends? da liegt die Nachtlast"). Null on rows without that input; the line is then absent, never a fabricated 0 (portal derivation + toggle rules in `frontend/portal/AGENTS.md`). **Der Preis, mit dem entschieden wurde, reist seit P0 "Textwahrheit" mit** (Scout `vp-netzbezug-nacht-s3` §6): `schedule.price_eur_mwh` ist der nackte SPOT, die Entscheidung fällt am komponierten Bezugspreis - der Kundensatz behauptete deshalb im NORMALFALL Unsinn ("Börsenpreis 21,2 wäre teurer als der Speicherwert 21,5", wahrer Bezug 32,5). `ScheduleSlotDto` trägt additiv `importPriceCtKwh`/`exportValueCtKwh`/`importPriceSource`, gefüllt von `optimizer/SchedulePricingService` als **reines Weiterreichen** der schon existierenden `SlotEconomics`-Rekomposition (die Admin-Diagnose nutzt sie seit je) - KEINE zweite Preisrechnung, und ohne persistierten Spot bleibt der Wert null statt erfunden. **Banked-value honesty (FK2, audit `vp-solver-xlsx-f2` §4.3):** on "bank days" the run stores energy into the next day and `savingsEur` alone reads negative although real value was banked, so the endpoint also returns `bankedValueEur` = persisted `terminal_value_eur_per_kwh` × (SoC_end − SoC_start) × capacity plus `socStartPct`/`socEndPct` (plan-START SoC reconstructed by reversing the first slot's solver dynamics with the battery asset's efficiency - the schedule table only persists slot-END SoC; all null when honestly not computable: pre-FK2 runs, no battery asset). The Fahrplan page renders it as one calm line ("davon in den Folgetag gespeichert: +X €" / sign-honest "aus dem Vortag entnommen: X €", pure `schedule.ts bankedValueLine`), glosses the savings KPI with an InfoTip, and `horizonHint` (pure, display-side) marks the pre-13-Uhr state where the horizon legitimately ends at today's midnight ("sobald die Börsenpreise für morgen vorliegen …") so the morning hold-tail is not mistaken for a broken plan.
- **Compose**: own **`optimize` profile** (not `feeds` - it consumes the feeds and publishes MQTT; not `edge` - it is cloud-side): `docker compose --profile feeds --profile optimize up -d --build` (without the feeds every site is skipped until prices land). Prod: the `optimization` image joins the pulled set in `docker-compose.prod.yml`. Env: `POSTGRES_*` (trusted backend role), `MQTT_HOST/PORT` (+ optional `MQTT_USERNAME/PASSWORD`), `OPTIMIZER_INTERVAL_SECONDS` (900), `OPTIMIZER_HORIZON_HOURS` (24), plus the Stage-1 tunables `OPTIMIZER_WEAR_COST_CT_PER_KWH` (4.0) / `OPTIMIZER_GRID_LIMIT_MAX_AGE_MINUTES` (60) / `OPTIMIZER_SOC_MAX_AGE_MINUTES` (120), the Stage-2 `OPTIMIZER_EEG_RATES_JSON` (feste-Vergütung schedule override) and the Stage-3 `OPTIMIZER_TERMINAL_VALUE_QUANTILE` (0.3) / `OPTIMIZER_TERMINAL_VALUE_CT_PER_KWH` (unset = derive per plan).
- **Tests** (offline, no Docker needed): `tests/test_solver.py` (economic behavior on synthetic curves: charge-cheap/discharge-expensive, zero savings on flat, spread-below-losses stays idle, power/SoC/grid-limit compliance, efficiency in the SoC dynamics, no simultaneous charge+discharge at negative prices, infeasible-cap fallback, and the Phase-3 curtailment economics - curtails at negative prices with a full battery, NEVER at positive prices, engages exactly in the negative half of a mixed curve, makes a tight export cap feasible while staying minimal, exists in the fallback build; note the negative-price tests tolerate battery cycling: paid import covering round-trip losses is genuinely profitable under symmetric spot pricing, not a bug - and these tests double as the P1 regression proof, since a default `OptimizationInput` without import/export series IS the symmetric model), `tests/test_pricing.py` (P1 unit: import per tarif_art, DV premium incl. §51 suspension/missing-month/merchant-mode guard, feste-Vergütung lookup incl. blending/degression/§51a/expiry/env override, Berlin month mapping, baseline economics, gather_inputs wiring over a fake psycopg), `tests/test_objective.py` (P1 through the solver: the captain's §2.2 reference scenario - PV to battery while the load imports cheap grid power, evening discharge; never import+export simultaneously even when export value > import price; the §2.3 retail-vs-spot-settled contrast pair on identical physics - retail self-consumes without export, spot-settled DV exports past its own load; the F1-Experiment-4 hold-for-the-late-load fix incl. the symmetric contrast; flat retail tariff kills grid arbitrage (F7); feste-Vergütung plant never curtails at negative spot while the DV twin does (F6); premium revenue in cost_eur/baseline; EEG never grid-charges under a juicy premium), `tests/test_contract.py` (payload validates against the frozen schema incl. formats; curtailing slots carry `pv_limit_kw >= 0`, non-curtailing slots OMIT it so pre-Phase-3 payloads stay byte-identical), `tests/test_engine.py` (cycle orchestration with fakes), `tests/test_fallback.py` (persistence reuse), `tests/test_active_model.py` (shadow-mode regression: only the active model's forecast rows are consumed; the promotion env flip switches them), `tests/test_wear.py` (wear threshold band, per-asset override beats default, persisted per-slot wear economics, zero-wear opt-out), `tests/test_freshness.py` (stale grid_limit → no cap + flagged, stale SoC → default, env-tunable windows, NULL asset wear → platform default), `tests/test_config.py` (env tunables fail loudly on garbage), `tests/test_terminal_value.py` (P3: the F3 scenario discharges its full battery into the evening peak and waits for it, a cheap end-of-horizon tail is held while the explicit-V_end=0 contrast dumps, merchant sells an early peak without a forced buy-back, flat-curve-stays-idle regression, the V_end derivation incl. quantile/override envs), `tests/test_backup_reserve.py` (P11: the floor arithmetic incl. below-reserve start + 100% pin, plans never below the configured reserve at any price, NULL = old 5% behavior, reserve-hard-vs-V_end-soft). NOTE for solver tests since P3: a plan may realize PRE-STORED energy at any price above the terminal anchor, so pure-cycling assertions must start at the SoC floor (`test_wear.py`/the small-spread test do). The api's `PortalApiTest.scheduleEndpointReturnsLatestPlanTenantScoped` proves the read path + RLS against real TimescaleDB/Keycloak.
- **Multi-entity co-optimizer basis (E4-Basis, v2 track; PR fm/vp2-e4-cooptimizer).** The solver generalization to N entities ships ALONGSIDE the untouched v1 path: `entities.py` (`CoOptimizationInput` with storages[]/producers[]/controllable_loads[] + the `from_v1_input` N=1 adapter + the `SitePlan` artifact; controllable loads are declared-but-empty-only until their E4 follow-up), `modules.py` (the former inline conditionals as DECLARED constraint/objective contributions selected per site - solar-only/§14a/feed-in/peak/reservation-stack; `build_co_model(..., modules=...)` is the strategy-node registration seam), `co_solver.py` (pure function, every v1 rule generalized by summation - ONE grid balance per connection point, per-entity SoC/binaries/wear/terminal value, EEG solar-only as a joint SUBSET constraint over the non-permitted storages). **The GOLDEN SUITE `tests/golden/` + `tests/test_golden_cooptimizer.py` is the cutover acceptance basis** - eight committed pilot-shaped scenarios (Excel reference day, EEG household, C&I peak shaving, DV negative prices, §14a incl. fallback build, kitchen-sink) solved through BOTH solvers and compared slot-by-slot (objective 1e-5 EUR, slots 1e-3 kW; fixtures are generator-pinned against drift) - read `tests/golden/README.md` BEFORE touching either solver, and never loosen its tolerances to make a change pass. **Schedule-2.0 shadow publisher** (`publisher_v2.py`): sites flagged via `VOLTPILOT_V2_PLAN_SITES` (env flip, default empty - no DB column, no site flagged in prod) additionally get a co-optimized per-entity plan retained on `ems/{t}/{s}/{d}/v2/plan` per `docs/contracts/v2/mqtt-schedule-2.0.schema.json` (E13a: v2 publishes, v1 controls - the 1.0 publisher is untouched for every site, a shadow failure only logs). D-8 discipline: `charge_from_grid_allowed` is ALWAYS explicit (absent = NOT allowed); a non-curtailing producer is OMITTED (release clears limits), a curtailing one keeps contiguity with `limit_pct: 100` no-ops. **DV-konformer Modus** = `CoOptimizationInput.dv_konform` (hard-disables grid-charge arbitrage for EVERY storage, most-restrictive-wins, consumption-side strategies stay active; mapping table onto the netzladen_erlaubt machinery in the `entities.py` docstring). Attribution groundwork (design only): `docs/attribution-three-pot.md` (green/grey/arbitrage three-pot extension of `EarningsRepository.arbitrageSplit` for the D6 Erlösbeteiligung - no earnings runtime change yet). Tests: `test_golden_cooptimizer.py`, `test_cooptimizer.py` (multi-entity: per-entity permissions, joint solar budget, DV-konform incl. peak-module-stays-active, efficiency-ordered cycling, curtailable-vs-must-run producers), `test_contract_v2.py` (payloads + the repo's v2 example fixtures validate, D-8 both values, release/no-op-limit rules, e2e kitchen-sink).

## Fahrplan-Warum backend (per-slot "why" facts: optimizer explain layer + schedule columns)

The optimizer computes + persists the honest per-slot decision facts of every plan (design scout `vp-fahrplan-why-design`, owner-approved D1-D6; the portal rendering is a separate later PR). **`voltpilot_optimization/explain.py`** runs AFTER `_extract_plan` on the still-live model: fix every binary to its MILP value AND relax its domain to `Reals` (BOTH required - `.fix()` alone leaves APPSI/HiGHS treating it as a MIP with no duals), fresh APPSI `Highs()` LP re-solve (~30-40 ms/cycle, objective drift <= 1e-13), then read the duals with the empirically verified sign conventions: **`λ_t = −dual(soc_dynamics[t])`** (value of a stored kWh at slot END, the "Wasserwert" - the EXACT number `SlotEconomics` previously approximated), **`π_t = dual(grid_balance[t])/dt`** (effective energy value at the connection point), **`μ_t = −dual(peak_epigraph[t]) − dual(peak_below_epigraph[t])`** (Leistungspreis allocation; Σ over slots+anchor = LP exactly, Σ ratchet duals = ratchet). Binding flags come from a primal-slack/bounds scan (no duals needed); the slot role is the §6 precedence rule-tree (`abregeln`/`reserve_halten`/`warten`/`pv_speichern`/`guenstig_laden`/`spitze_kappen`/`verkaufen`/`eigenverbrauch`), deadbands = the shared `schedule.ts`/`SlotEconomics` constants (third copy in explain.py - change together).

- **Safety invariants (non-negotiable):** the committed plan is BYTE-IDENTICAL with explain on/off (proven per golden scenario); the LP re-solve MUTATES the model so it runs strictly after plan extraction and its solution is never loaded back (`load_solution=False`, no `load_vars()`); ANY explain exception (incl. a garbage flag value) warn-logs and ships the plan with all why-fields None - explain can never sink a plan; flag `OPTIMIZER_EXPLAIN_ENABLED` (default true) is the instant off-switch; the why NEVER reaches MQTT (publisher builds payloads field-by-field). The Ersparnis-Simulation passes `optimize(..., explain_plan=False)` (365 solves/job would pay ~11 s for discarded fields).
- **Maintenance coupling (§11.6):** whoever adds a model constraint MUST extend `explain.KNOWN_CONSTRAINTS` + the binding scan/role tree - `explain()` REFUSES unmapped constraints (plan then ships why-less), and `test_explain.py`'s inventory guard fails at dev time.
- **Data contract (§5.1, api migration `V20260723030000` + mirror `infra/local/timescale/04-schedule.sql`):** typed nullable `schedule` columns `slot_role` TEXT, `slot_flags` TEXT (CSV; empty tuple persists NULL), `stored_value_ct_kwh` (λ, rounded 0.1 ct - degeneracy rounding), `grid_value_ct_kwh` (π), `peak_pressure_eur_kw` (μ), `fallback_14a` BOOLEAN (run-level, repeated per row - the terminal_value pattern; TRUE = advisory no-§14a build). NULL everywhere = pre-feature/explain-off - consumers render exactly today's view, never a fabricated explanation. Served additively on `ScheduleSlotDto`/`SchedulePlanDto` (customer `GET /sites/{id}/schedule`) and on the admin diagnostics, where the persisted exact `stored_value_ct_kwh` replaces the approximation (`storedEnergyValueIsApproximation` is false only when EVERY rendered slot carries it).
- **Portal consumer - "a 'nothing to do' must name its true cause or stay silent" (2026-07-27).** The Fahrplan's idle sentence used to be a blanket fallback asserting "die Preisunterschiede lohnen kein Laden und Entladen" while inspecting NO price - which is what hid the flat-tariff freeze above for as long as it lasted (idle over a 20 ct spread, blaming the spread). `schedule.ts` `idleReason` now derives it from these persisted facts - dominant `slot_role` of the IDLE slots, `reserve_backup`/`reserve_peak` flags, `stored_value_ct_kwh` vs the window's best price - and returns **null when the optimizer recorded nothing**, in which case both consumers (`planInsightParts` on the Fahrplan chart, `planSentence` on the Anlagen-Seite mini preview) state the observation and claim NO cause. An idle stretch under a role the portal vocabulary does not know yet also makes no claim, so extending the role set can never invent copy. Pinned by `src/schedule.test.ts`.
- **Tests:** `tests/test_explain.py` (stationarity identities η·λ=π+wear / λ=η·(π−wear) within the ε-tie-break tolerance, Σμ=LP+ratchet exact, role vectors per scenario, fallback marking, plan-byte-identical on/off, explain-failure-never-sinks, constraint-inventory guard); `PortalApiTest`/`AdminApiTest` pin the read side + null degradation. The golden suite is the primary safety gate and stays untouched.

## Price-aware in-slot trim: the cloud prices, the edge enforces (2026-07-30)

The 15-min setpoint stands for the whole quarter hour, but PV and house load move every second - so a forecast shortfall was silently covered from the GRID. Captain observation at Anlage Pilsting (30.07., 14:44): PV 15,3 · Haus 7,6 · Batterie lädt 11,1 · **Netzbezug 3,4 kW** at a 3,3 ct spot against a 22 ct evening, i.e. the purchase was economically RIGHT - which is exactly why "just charge the surplus" is the WRONG fix (that is the price-blind Eigenverbrauchslogik, the same category error as the fixed night-emptying bug with the sign flipped). The answer is CONDITIONAL, and the split is architectural law: **THE CLOUD DECIDES WHETHER A SLOT IS EXPENSIVE, THE EDGE ONLY LIMITS.** The edge evaluates no price, ever (one price truth - the `pricing.py`/`SlotEconomics` discipline).

- **The rule (`services/optimization/voltpilot_optimization/slot_trim.py`)**: the marginal willingness to pay for one more AC kWh charged in slot t is `WTP_t = eta*λ_t − wear`, where **λ_t is the model's OWN marginal value of stored energy** (the LP dual the explain layer already extracts and persists as `stored_value_ct_kwh`). A grid purchase for the battery is uneconomic iff `import_price_t > WTP_t + margin` (`SLOT_TRIM_MARGIN_CT_PER_KWH` 0.5 ct - it swallows the 0.1 ct degeneracy rounding of the persisted λ AND keeps a hairline case untrimmed). A plain threshold ("above X ct") was deliberately rejected: it is wrong at the next price regime, whereas this test IS the model's optimality condition and self-adjusts. Sanity check on the observed case: WTP ≈ 28 ct ≫ 14,6 ct import → NOT uneconomic → Pilsting keeps doing exactly what it did.
