- **Emission** (`solver._charge_from_surplus_only`, stamped in `_with_explanation` next to the why-fields; `PlanSlot.charge_from_surplus_only`): only on slots whose plan actually COMMANDS a charge (the `pv_limit_kw` discipline - every other payload stays byte-identical), plus a consistency guard that never flags a slot the plan itself grid-charges in. λ comes from explain, so `OPTIMIZER_EXPLAIN_ENABLED=false` (or the new `OPTIMIZER_SLOT_TRIM_ENABLED=false` kill switch) degrades to today's behavior.
- **Contract**: the OPTIONAL per-slot boolean **`charge_from_surplus_only`** in `docs/contracts/mqtt-schedule.schema.json` = the edge's DUTY to clamp commanded CHARGE to the MEASURED surplus `max(pv − load, 0)`. Distinct from the top-level `grid_charge_allowed`: that one is REGULATORY (EEG, charge ≤ measured PV PRODUCTION, house may import in parallel) and the Go edge fails SAFE on it; this one is ECONOMIC (charge ≤ measured SURPLUS, so the battery causes no import at all) and is deliberately **FAIL-OPEN** - a missing economic hint must never reshape dispatch, and an inferred restriction would destroy real arbitrage. Most restrictive wins; neither ever widens anything.
- **Edge (`edge-app/core/internal/guards/slottrim.go` `PriceTrimmer`, applied in `applySetpoint` after Clamp/arbitration and before the peak guard)**: restrict-only on charge, asymmetric hysteresis (engage immediately - a passing cloud is what must not be bought; release only after the plan setpoint has comfortably fit the surplus for `TrimReleaseDwell` 90 s), the applied cap follows a SHRINKING surplus at once and a growing one only in `TrimStepKw` steps (stable register writes). **Safety by algebra:** after a trim the predicted grid power is `max(load − pv, 0) ≥ 0`, so the site is never pushed into export → the §14a export bound and any feed-in cap are untouchable, and the import bound was applied to a value the trim only reduces further; the result lives strictly in `[0, kw]`, so the rated band + SoC window hold trivially and FK3 (looser) can never be widened. Unknown pv/load → INACTIVE (never regulate blind - the economic-guard convention, cf. PeakShave). The self-consumption fallback follows pv − load, i.e. the surplus itself, so the trim is a no-op there.
- **A trim is a DELIBERATE limitation, never a failed write:** the TRIMMED value is what gets published, so the register readback matches it and the PR #280 confirmation logic cannot read it as "Sollwert nicht übernommen". It is surfaced as its own state (`state.TrimInfo` → the `:8484` control card's reason line), because an unnamed limitation reads as a defect.
- **Tests:** `test_slot_trim.py` (the rule as economic properties with the captain's live numbers as the anchor; through the real solver: a flat-retail plant flags EVERY planned charge slot while a cheap-night purchase for an expensive evening stays untouched; flag off / explain off; setpoints byte-identical with the trim off), `test_contract.py` (additive/omitted + the committed fixtures both ways + fixture-vs-publisher), Go `guards/slottrim_test.go` (surplus hold, no-surplus stops instead of buying, discharge untouched, blind-inactive, FK3-tighter, §14a-export-safe, no flapping, wiggle-stable cap) + `agent/slot_trim_test.go` (expensive/cheap/legacy/stale/no-reading through `applySetpoint`, published==state) + `plan/plan_test.go` (explicit-true only, active-slot semantics, the committed fixtures), edge JS `web/jstest/ui.test.js` (the reason line), portal `control.test.ts`.

## In-slot load following: the DISCHARGE mirror of the trim (2026-07-30, P1)

The same quarter-hour gap with the sign flipped, and the one that cost real money - the Pilsting NIGHT (scout report `firstmate/data/vp-netzbezug-nacht-s3`, §6 P1). The plan discharges exactly what it FORECASTS the house to draw, so an under-forecast quarter is bought from the grid: measured live 21:22 MESZ, plan −4,332 kW into a **7,117 kW** house = **2,79 kW at ~32,5 ct** while the battery sat at **77 % SoC**, and the plan - believing the night smaller than it was - carried ~15 kWh unused into the morning. **≈ 4,9 EUR in ONE night.** Same architectural law as the trim: **the cloud decides the economics of the slot, the edge only enforces against MEASURED values** (the edge evaluates no price, ever).

- **The rule (same module `slot_trim.py`)**: covering one AC kWh of house load from the battery COSTS `λ_t/eta + wear` (delivering 1 AC kWh drains `1/eta` stored kWh) and SAVES `import_price_t`, so it is economic iff `import_price_t > λ_t/eta + wear + margin` (the shared `SLOT_TRIM_MARGIN_CT_PER_KWH`). This is STRICTLY tighter than the charge-side test, so **the two duties can never contradict each other** (`test_load_follow.py` pins that as a property sweep). Again the model's own optimality condition, not a threshold.
- **Emission** (`solver._cover_load_from_battery`, stamped in `_with_explanation`; `PlanSlot.cover_load_from_battery`): only where the plan actually COMMANDS a discharge **and** does not itself plan an import (`grid_kw <= 0`) - i.e. exactly the report's "role `eigenverbrauch`, geplantes Netz ≈ 0" shape. Where the plan deliberately buys (the cheap hours, role `warten`) the flag is NOT set, so **the price arbitrage is untouched**. Own kill-switch `OPTIMIZER_LOAD_FOLLOW_ENABLED` (deliberately NOT shared with the trim - the two push the setpoint in opposite directions on a control path, so an operator must be able to stop one without losing the other); no explain layer → no flag.
- **Contract**: the OPTIONAL per-slot boolean **`cover_load_from_battery`** = the edge's permission to RAISE commanded DISCHARGE to the MEASURED deficit `max(load − pv, 0)`. FAIL-OPEN like its twin (an inferred duty would be the price-blind Eigenverbrauchslogik). Fixtures `mqtt-schedule.valid.cover-load.json` + `…invalid.cover-load-not-boolean.json`.
- **Edge (`guards/loadfollow.go` `LoadFollower`, in `applySetpoint` right after the trim, before the peak guard)**: it **CALLS `PeakShave(kw, 0, …)`** rather than re-deriving the bounds, so rated band / SoC floor / never-raise come from ONE proven piece of arithmetic - "restrict-to-zero-grid". **Safety by algebra:** where it bites the predicted grid power is exactly **0**, so the site is never pushed into export (§14a export bound + feed-in cap untouchable) and the import bound was applied to a value it only reduces further. It only ever DEEPENS an existing discharge - a commanded CHARGE is never flipped (a direction change is not a magnitude correction). The plan-carried **peak reserve raises its SoC floor** (ordinary load covering is exactly what the reserve must survive - the fallback's rule); peak DEFENSE may still go below it. Unknown pv/load → INACTIVE. The self-consumption fallback already follows pv − load, so it is a no-op there. **Deliberate deviation from the trim: no step-limited follower on the applied value** - both directions here are load following, and holding a stale deeper discharge while the deficit SHRINKS would push the site into export.
- **A raise is a DELIBERATE correction, never a failed write** (the same PR #280 lesson): the FOLLOWED value is published, so the readback matches it. Surfaced as `state.FollowInfo` → `control.js VPControl.deriveFollow` → the same `#ctrlReason` line ("Deckt den gemessenen Hausverbrauch aus dem Speicher …", NORMAL mode).
- **Tests:** `test_load_follow.py` (rule properties incl. the never-contradict sweep; through the REAL solver the report's night curve marks its `eigenverbrauch` slots while a cheap-hour purchase stays unmarked; both switches independent; setpoints byte-identical with the flag off), `test_contract.py`, Go `guards/loadfollow_test.go` + `agent/load_follow_test.go` + `plan/plan_test.go`, edge JS `web/jstest/ui.test.js`. Verified end to end on real bytes: the real solver's payload for the report's night → `plan.Parse` → `applySetpoint` turns the plan's −4,302 kW into **−7,087 kW**, predicted grid **0,000 kW** (live: +2,755 kW).

## E2 flow platform core (edge): arbitration runtime + flowc compiler + flow deployment

The v2 track's edge runtime slice (branch fm/vp2-e2-flow-core; contracts `docs/contracts/v2/` are BINDING - E2's additive clarifications are logged in that README's decision log). Details + proof map in `edge-app/AGENTS.md` §"E2 flow platform core"; the essentials:
- **Desired arbitration** (`edge-app/core/internal/desired` + `agent/arbitration.go`): flows publish WISHES on `edge/entities/{id}/desired`; the core arbitrates (D-4 classes, D-6 holder-keeps conflicts, D-5 override ≤ 4 h, D-7 required TTL), clamps the winner through the per-entity registry guard chain COMPOSED with the v1 device limits (most restrictive wins; stage-attributed reasons via `guards.ClampTraced`), and ALONE publishes the retained `…/command` + arbitration events. The winning holder also drives the physical v1 `edge/setpoint` write; the registry failsafe stays with the v1 fallback computation. The v2 plan (`internal/plan2`, retained `…/v2/plan`, v1 staleness semantics) injects class-`market` desires - a fresh v1 plan keeps controlling the battery (shadow phase, "v1 controls").
- **flowc** (`edge-app/nodered/flowc`): deterministic flow-graph→Node-RED compiler (catalog-only nodes, whitelisted template codegen, `@vp-flow` tab marker, RFC 8785 `content_hash`). CROSS-LANGUAGE LOCKSTEP: `flowc/canonicalize.js` ⟷ `edge-app/core/internal/flowdeploy/jcs.go` share `flowc/jcs-vectors.json`, and the committed `flowc/testdata/*.artifact.json` must verify through the Go deployer - never edit one side alone. vp-palette 0.2.0 adds `vp-desired`/`vp-entity-read`/`vp-feed`/`vp-notify`.
- **Flow deployment** (`edge-app/core/internal/flowdeploy` + `agent/flows.go`): retained `…/v2/flows` set → hash/semver-gate/capability verification (palette version read LIVE from NR `GET /nodes`) → `@vp-flow` tabs via the NR Admin API → heartbeat `flows` acks; persistence + periodic reconcile self-heal. Core env: `VP_NODERED_ADMIN_URL`/`VP_NODERED_USER`/`VP_NODERED_PASSWORD` (unset = honest `error` acks naming the setting).
- **Reseed coexistence (D-12)**: `edge-app/nodered/reseed-merge-flows.js` + the entrypoint merge preserve `@vp-flow` tabs across template re-seeds, proven against the REAL image (`reseed-flows.docker.test.sh` - the #117/#119 discipline).
- **September-Gate rig**: `edge-app/test/e2e-v2-compose.sh` (own project/ports; pre-builds via plain `docker build` for old-buildx hosts) proves registry push → flowc artifact → NR → desired → arbitration → guards → certified sunspec write → register readback, plus clamp/conflict/override/staleness/coexistence (spec P1-P7).

## Portal-Admin optimizer surface (diagnostics + config, `services/api` `optimizer/` package)

Backend for the admin "understand + tune the optimizer" GUI (design: firstmate `vp-admin-optimizer-ui-design`; the React page is the follow-up task). Three `platform-admin`-gated routes under `/api/v1/admin/sites/{siteId}/...` (`AdminOptimizerController`), all reading/writing THROUGH the RLS-scoped app datasource via the `X-Tenant-Id` switcher (no BYPASSRLS; no tenant selected / wrong tenant = 404). The what-if re-optimize (design §4.3) is deliberately DEFERRED - config writes change what the optimizer's next 15-min cycle reads, nothing triggers a solve.

- **`GET .../optimizer-diagnostics?generatedAt=&date=`** (default = latest run; unknown timestamp 404; `availableRuns` lists ONE Europe/Berlin day's runs - `date=YYYY-MM-DD` picks the day and without `generatedAt` shows that day's newest run, a day without runs = empty well-formed body, never 404; `firstRunDate`/`lastRunDate` bound the UI date picker - the hypertable keeps every run, the former newest-30 list only reached ~7.5 h back): the run's `schedule` slots plus the per-slot €-decomposition in ct/kWh - `solverPriceCtKwh` (bare spot), `importPriceCtKwh`/`exportValueCtKwh` (the REAL P1 pricing), `wearCostEur`/`wearCostCtKwh` (from the PERSISTED `schedule.wear_cost_eur`, real P2 - not an estimate), `valueOfStoredEnergyCtKwh` (forward best-use price × one-way η minus half-wear - an APPROXIMATION, flagged run-level as `storedEnergyValueIsApproximation`; the exact number would be the MILP's SoC shadow price, not persisted), `decisionLabel` (`solarladen|netzladen|entladen|ruhe`, the server twin of `schedule.ts chargeKind`, same 0.05 kW deadband) and a composed German `whyText`. Null wherever honestly not computable (no persisted spot, pre-P2 rows, battery-less site) - never a fake 0. The §14a limit a run used is NOT persisted, so it is not returned (known gap); forecast-model lifecycle comes from `forecast-quality`.
- **Pricing consistency is the load-bearing invariant:** `optimizer/SlotEconomics.java` mirrors `services/optimization/voltpilot_optimization/pricing.py` rule-for-rule (tarif_art branching; DV Marktprämie `max(aw − Monatsmarktwert, 0)` suspended at negative spot + missing-month = no premium; merchant mode = bare spot despite configured EEG remuneration; eigenverbrauch feste Vergütung incl. §51a and 20-year expiry), and `optimizer/EegRates.java` is the Java twin of `config.py`'s `DEFAULT_EEG_RATE_SCHEDULE` + tranche blending - `EegRatesTest` pins the SAME vectors as `test_pricing.py`, so a schedule change must land on both sides. The api reads the same `OPTIMIZER_EEG_RATES_JSON` env.
- **`GET`/`PUT .../optimizer-config`**: platform defaults vs. per-site/per-asset overrides vs. effective values. Overrides (NULL = platform default, additive nullable columns): `asset.wear_cost_ct_per_kwh` (V20260710000000), `asset.soc_min_pct`/`soc_max_pct` (**V20260710020000**, the formerly hard-coded 5-95% band from `domain.py` - now per-battery-asset, read by `inputs.load_battery_sites` with an inconsistent-band fall-back-and-warn), `site.backup_reserve_soc_pct` (V20260710010000). The PUT is FULL-REPRESENTATION (null clears; battery fields without a battery asset = 409; effective min ≥ max = 400). The pre-existing site levers (netzladen/plant_kind/tariff/anzulegender Wert) are echoed read-only - they stay on `PUT /api/v1/sites/{id}`. Terminal-value config is read-only (env-level).
- **The api mirrors the optimizer's env** via `voltpilot.optimizer.*` (`OptimizerProperties`): BOTH composes pass `OPTIMIZER_WEAR_COST_CT_PER_KWH` / `OPTIMIZER_TERMINAL_VALUE_*` / `OPTIMIZER_EEG_RATES_JSON` to the api service too - keep api + optimization env in sync (the `VOLTPILOT_ACTIVE_*_MODEL` discipline) or the panel displays defaults the solver is not using.
- **Tests:** `EegRatesTest` + `SlotEconomicsTest` (pure unit, always run - pricing vectors, wear derivation, stored-value math, decision label, whyText, null discipline); `AdminApiTest.optimizerDiagnosticsDecomposeSlotsWithRealTariffAndRemuneration` (hand-computed DV dynamic-tariff + Marktprämie/§51 and EV fest-tariff + feste-Vergütung slots, run selection, auth/tenancy incl. wrong-tenant 404) + `optimizerConfigMergesDefaultsWithOverridesAndWritesThem` (defaults/override/effective merge, writes land on the exact columns `inputs.py` reads, clear-to-default, band validation, battery-less 409, auth); optimization `test_freshness.py` SoC-band section (NULL = defaults, configured band, inconsistent-band fallback).

## Peak Shaving / Lastspitzenkappung (PS-1 + PS-2, cloud side)

Captain go 2026-07-16 (spec: scout `vp-battery-models-b9` report Teil 3): RLM sites pay a **Leistungspreis** (€/kW per billing period) on the period's highest 15-min mean grid IMPORT; the battery shaves it. Cloud planning, the edge peak guard (PS-3) and the customer-facing reporting (PS-4) are all BUILT - see the last two bullets.

- **Master data (api migration `V20260716020000`):** nullable `site.leistungspreis_eur_kw` (**non-NULL IS the module flag** - the max_feed_in philosophy, no separate boolean), `site.abrechnung_leistung` (`jahr`|`monat`, NOT NULL default `jahr`, Europe/Berlin calendar periods), nullable `site.peak_reserve_soc_pct` (PS-2), plus `schedule.peak_target_kw` (run-level fact repeated per slot, the terminal_value pattern; mirrored in `infra/local/timescale/04-schedule.sql`). **Configuration is ADMIN-ONLY** (captain: VoltPilot richtet vertragsnahe Module ein): the three fields are written EXCLUSIVELY via the platform-admin `PUT .../optimizer-config` (`peakShaving` block, full-representation - null LP = module off, null abrechnung resets to `jahr`) and echoed read-only on `SiteDto`; they deliberately do NOT exist on Create/UpdateSiteRequest.
- **`peak_so_far` is computed FRESH per optimizer cycle, never stored** (`inputs._peak_so_far_kw`, module-active sites only): `max(grid_import_kwh)*4.0` from `telemetry_rollup_15m` since the Berlin period start (`billing_period_start`), blended (max) with the RUNNING quarter-hour's raw `avg(greatest(power_kw,0))` because the rollup refresh lags ≤15 min. **Plausibility gate** (`plausible_peak`, `config.PEAK_SPIKE_FACTOR` 3.0): the period's top bucket is discarded for the second-highest when it exceeds 3× it - one unfiltered legacy-edge telemetry spike must not anchor the whole year; a real recurring peak (similar top buckets) always survives.
- **MILP (`solver.py`, in BOTH builds - economic, no new infeasibility path, §14a fallback untouched):** epigraph `peak >= grid_import_t`, `peak >= peak_so_far`, objective `+ LP*(peak − peak_so_far)` (exact for the period; no dt - it is a per-POWER price) `+ peak_ratchet_eur_per_kw(LP) * peak_below` where `peak_below` is the plain horizon peak. **The ratchet weight is `min(3%·LP, 0.30 €/kW)`** - the cap is load-bearing and was proven necessary: uncapped 3% of a Jahres-LP (100-200 €/kW → 3-6 €/kW per plan) PROVABLY dominates real arbitrage (a 400 €/MWh spread earns only ~0.6 €/kW of charge power - the solver covered cheap load instead of exporting at 500). The report's binding requirements (wear-scale, "nie echte Arbitrage dominieren") win over its "z. B. 2-5%" example; derivation in `config.py`. Export is NEVER in the epigraph (import-only instrument).
- **PS-2 reserve:** `BatteryParams.peak_reserve_pct` joins the reservation STACK via the same `soc_floor_kwh` machinery as the P11 backup reserve (incl. below-floor-start relaxation): each reservation is an ABSOLUTE SoC floor, `max` binds (technical < backup < peak-reserve) - NOT additive; hard at any price.
- **Publish/persist:** module-active plans carry the ADDITIVE optional top-level contract fields **`grid_import_limit_kw`** (= the solved `peak`, the future edge peak-guard target) and **`peak_reserve_soc_pct`** (`mqtt-schedule.schema.json`, schema_version stays 1.0; absent = no module, old edges ignore both safely - documented x-failsafe); `schedule.peak_target_kw` persists the target per run (NULL = module off, never fabricated).
- **Tests:** optimization `tests/test_peak_shaving.py` (hand-computed: hold-vs-economically-break the peak, ratchet shaves below the anchor after a torn peak / never blocks a profitable cycle, period-start flattening, export unpenalized, reserve stack + hard floor, §14a compose + fallback-build shaving, EEG discharge-only, contract fields, billing-period/gate/wiring units) + `test_freshness.py` column resolution; api `AdminApiTest.peakShavingModuleIsAdminConfiguredAndEchoedReadOnlyOnTheSite` (writes land on the exact columns `inputs.py` reads, SiteDto read-only echo, customer path cannot touch them, validation, authz/tenancy).
- **PS-3 edge peak guard (`edge-app/core`, the local closed loop - the billing peak is a 15-min MEAN, so a stochastic load jump at :07 must be counter-regulated INSIDE the running quarter hour; the 15-min cloud MPC can only plan peaks).** `plan.Parse` reads the two optional contract fields (validated; reserve only alongside a valid target; nil = module off = byte-for-byte pre-PS, persisted with the plan across reboots); the accessors `PeakImportLimit()`/`PeakReserveSoc()` are DELIBERATELY staleness-independent - on a dead cloud link the guard keeps defending the LAST known target (contract x-failsafe amended for PS-3; safe because restrict-only). `guards.PeakTracker` integrates the gated composite `power_kw` (import-only - RLM meters register per direction, export never offsets; hold-last between samples, wall-clock :00/:15/:30/:45 quarters via `Truncate` - Berlin offset is whole hours so no tzdata; >2-min measurement gap = tracker inactive, never regulate blind) and projects the remaining-quarter import allowance; `guards.PeakShave` runs AFTER `Clamp` in `applySetpoint` (BOTH modes) and only ever LOWERS the setpoint toward a non-negative import target, respecting rated discharge + the SoC floor - so §14a (both directions), EEG solar-only and the band are provably never violated. **Fallback composition:** ordinary self-consumption discharge stops at the plan-carried `peak_reserve_soc_pct`; peak DEFENSE may discharge below it down to `SocMinPct` (that is what the reserve is for); a fresh plan's own setpoints are never reserve-floored (the cloud plans its own stack). `/api/state` additively exposes `peak_target_kw`/`peak_reserve_soc_pct`/`peak_guard_active`/`peak_quarter_mean_kw` → the `:8484` "Betrieb" card (`static/betrieb.js`, //go:embed - rebuild core after edits). Tests: `guards/peakguard_test.go` (tracker boundaries/gap/backfill/allowance math, PeakShave incl. compliance-composition proof), `plan` parse/round-trip, `agent/peak_guard_test.go` (reserve floor, defense-below-reserve, legacy byte-for-byte, schedule-mode shave, blind-inactive, telemetry plumbing), `web` (card structure + envelope), e2e-compose publishes the fields.
- **PS-4 reporting ("Vermiedene Spitze: X kW × Y €/kW = Z €", read-only, no schema change):** `GET /api/v1/earnings` carries a per-site **`peakShaving`** block - present exactly when the module is active (non-NULL Leistungspreis), RANGE-INDEPENDENT like `expectedMarketValue*` (always the RUNNING Berlin billing period). `repo/PeakShavingRepository` computes per period from `telemetry_rollup_15m`: measured peak = `max(grid_import_kwh)*4`, counterfactual no-battery peak = `max(greatest(import − export + discharge − charge, 0))*4` (== `max(0, load − pv)` per the power-balance identity - the same plant, battery idle; approximations documented in its Javadoc), `avoidedKw = max(0, baseline − measured)` (floor: a battery-CAUSED peak reads 0, never negative), `avoidedEur = avoidedKw × LP` NOT pro-rated (the standing saving; final once the period closes; current contract price applied to the 12-period `history` too). Buckets without `grid_import_kwh` are skipped; a module site without measurements gets config echo + null peaks (never fabricated). Portal: the Optimierung card's proof rows (`lastspitzenProof` in `moduleSurface.ts`, InfoTip "ohne Speichereinsatz" on the Vermiedene-Spitze row; `OptimierungSection` fetches `api.earnings('month')` fail-soft) + ONE calm `vermiedeneSpitzeLine` in the `AnlageHero` money hero (hidden below the 0.05 kW deadband). A Peak-Verlauf chart (measured vs. `schedule.peak_target_kw` per period) is deliberately NOT built yet - the `history` data for it is already in the response. Proven by `PortalApiTest.earningsExposeThePeakShavingProofPerBillingPeriod` + portal `moduleSurface.test.ts`/`OptimierungSection.test.tsx`.

## Historie (history/reporting: `services/api` history package + portal "Historie" page)

The customer-facing history view (captain's decisions: **money lens leads**, private customers first, core value **Nachvollziehbarkeit** - the customer can see and verify what the system did and why).
Read-only feature: one migration, one endpoint, one portal page.

- **Telemetry rollups instead of continuous aggregates.** TimescaleDB **hard-refuses caggs on hypertables with row-level security** ("cannot create continuous aggregate on hypertable with row security", verified on 2.17.2), and `telemetry` has FORCE RLS.
  So api migration **`V20260701030000`** builds the same outcome from primitives that respect the RLS spine: plain rollup **hypertables** `telemetry_rollup_15m/1h/1d` (per-bucket energies pv/load/grid-import/grid-export/battery-charge/battery-discharge kWh + SoC min/max/last + `n_samples`), RLS-scoped exactly like telemetry, refreshed by a TimescaleDB **background job** (`add_job`, 15-min interval) running an idempotent upsert cascade (raw -> 15m -> 1h -> 1d) over a 7-day trailing window as the job owner = the Flyway superuser (bypasses RLS to aggregate all tenants).
  The migration backfills all raw history once; data arriving >7 days late needs a manual `CALL refresh_telemetry_rollups(<since>)`.
  Raw `telemetry` stays untouched (no retention - the raw corpus is future ML training data).
  Semantics live in the migration header: energy = avg(power over the bucket's samples) x bucket hours with per-sample import/export split; actual battery power derived from the power balance (`battery_kw = power_kw - load_kw + pv_power_kw`); daily buckets are **Europe/Berlin** days (the v1 platform timezone, `HistoryRange.ZONE`).
- **Endpoint** `GET /api/v1/sites/{siteId}/history?range=day|week|month|year&at=<date>` (SiteController -> `history/HistoryService` -> RLS-scoped `HistoryRepository`; foreign site 404, bad range 400; in `openapi.yaml`).
  Day = live 15-min buckets straight from raw telemetry (never behind the rollup refresh); week/month/year = the rollups, with per-bucket grid cost merged from the 15m-rollup x `day_ahead_prices` join.
  Totals formulas documented on `HistoryTotalsDto`: grid cost = 15-min import energy x matching day-ahead price; battery savings = sum(baseline - cost) over persisted `schedule` slots taking the **latest run per slot** (`DISTINCT ON (time) ... ORDER BY generated_at DESC`, so overlapping MPC runs never double-count; **null** where no plan exists); Autarkiegrad = 1 - import/consumption; Eigenverbrauchsquote = (pv - export)/pv.
  **Totals honesty (audit V2/X1/H3/H8, 2026-07-24):** the four ENERGY sums follow the cost fields' discipline - **null, never 0**, when not a single bucket carried that channel (a 0-bucket day used to return a confident `pvGenerationKwh: 0.0` next to the board's honest "noch keine Daten"), and a ratio whose numerator or denominator is unknown stays null. The ex-ante plan sum is served as **`batterySavingsPlannedEur`** (`batterySavingsEur` survives ONE release as a same-valued deprecated alias) because it is NOT measured money - the measured counterpart is `savedEur` on `/api/v1/earnings` and the two legitimately differ ~8x on the same plant/period, so any surface rendering it must say "geplant". `gridCostEur` is the REAL supply cost since Stufe 3 of the structured Bezugspreis: import energy × the same `SlotEconomics.importPriceCtSql` composition the optimizer plans with (bare spot only without any price data - byte-identical to before); the additive **`tarifArt`** + **`tarifPriced`** label it honestly (`tarifPriced=false` = the number must read "zu Börsenpreisen", true = "bewertet zu Ihrem Stromtarif"; a flat tariff prices even unpriced buckets).
  Day range additionally returns the **Tagesprotokoll** (`history/Tagesprotokoll`, pure functions: battery charge/discharge windows above a 0.1 kW noise threshold with energy-weighted avg price and avoided-cost EUR, PV peak, price extremes; plain-German server-formatted texts) and the **plan-vs-actual** trajectory (latest persisted plan value per 15-min slot).
- **Bucket -> Preis geht NUR über `repo/PriceSlots`, nie wieder über ein LATERAL pro Bucket (gemessener Defekt, Scout `vp-historie-konzept-t4` §5, behoben 2026-07-30).** Die alte Zuordnung `LEFT JOIN LATERAL (... ORDER BY (p.resolution = 'PT15M') DESC, p.ts DESC LIMIT 1)` sortiert nach einem **Ausdruck**, also kann Postgres `idx_day_ahead_prices_zone_ts` nicht zum vorzeitigen Abbruch nutzen: das `LIMIT 1` greift erst nach dem Sortieren, jeder Bucket las und verwarf ~1.400 Preiszeilen (`loops=3625`, 111.985 Buffer-Hits, **1,2 s** für ein Jahresfenster - auf dem Demo-Stack reproduziert), und dieselbe Form lief **fünfmal pro `/earnings`-Anfrage**. `PriceSlots` baut die Preisreihe **einmal** je (Zone, Fenster) als `MATERIALIZED`-CTE `price_slot(bidding_zone, slot, price_eur_mwh)` - PT60M auf Viertelstunden aufgefaltet, Gewinner je Slot per `DISTINCT ON` mit **denselben** Sortierschlüsseln - danach ist es ein Equi-Join: **~9-18 ms**, und die Kosten wachsen nicht mehr mit der Zahl der Buckets. Drei Dinge, die man dabei kennen muss: (1) die einzige Annahme ist ein **viertelstunden-ausgerichtetes Join-Raster** (Rollup-Buckets, `time_bucket`, `forecast.time` erfüllen sie; die Preiszeilen selbst dürfen beliebig liegen - ihr erster abgedeckter Slot wird abgeleitet); (2) die Auffaltung nutzt bewusst ein 4-zeiliges `VALUES` statt `generate_series` - eine SRF im LATERAL kostet ~30 µs Executor-Setup **pro Preiszeile** und misst 100 ms, wo das `VALUES` 3 ms misst; (3) das Fenster-Prädikat rechnet auf einem Bind-Parameter und braucht deshalb `?::timestamptz`, sonst hält Postgres den Platzhalter für ein `interval`. Semantik-Abnahme ist `PriceSlotEqualityTest` (Testcontainers): das alte LATERAL steht dort wörtlich als Orakel und wird Slot für Slot gegen `PriceSlots` verglichen - nur PT15M, nur PT60M, gemischt, Lücken, unausgerichtete `ts`, unbekannte Auflösung, `ts DESC`-Gleichstand, und eine Preiszeile die VOR dem Fenster beginnt (der Wächter für die 75-Minuten-Untergrenze).
- **Portal surface**: **TWO worlds, two routes** (captain structure H1, 2026-07-30; concept `data/vp-historie-konzept-t4`) — `#/anlage/{id}/messwerte` (`pages/MesswerteSection.tsx`, BASE: the energy sums + the one multi-series chart carrying PV/Haus/Netz/Batterie/Ladestand + the Messwerte-Explorer as a disclosure) and `#/anlage/{id}/erloese` (`pages/ErloeseSection.tsx`, MODE-BOUND on `surface.ts` `erloes-historie`: Stromkosten, the GEPLANTE Speicher-Ersparnis, the Speicher-&-Preis proof, Tagesprotokoll). `#/anlage/{id}/historie` redirects to `messwerte` **preserving `?m=`/`z=`/`at=`**. Both share ONE skeleton (world head with an honesty badge + one-click world switch that carries the period, sticky time bar, cards, foot card) and ONE fetch+cache (`historyCache.ts`). Seit PR D trägt die Zeit-Leiste zusätzlich den **Zeitraum-Sprung** (natives Datums-/Wochen-/Monatsfeld + Jahresauswahl + der wiederverwendete `MonthStrip` ohne erfundene Werte), das **Δ zur Vorperiode** (ein zweiter Abruf desselben Endpunkts durch denselben Cache — nie gegen eine erfundene Null, gewertet nur wo die Richtung eindeutig ist) und die **Datenabdeckung**. Derivation rules, honesty badges (Gemessen · Bewertet · Geplant) and the measured CSS/ECharts footguns: `frontend/portal/AGENTS.md` "Die Historie sind ZWEI WELTEN".
- **Datenabdeckung des Zeitraums (F4/P7, additiv auf der `/history`-Antwort):** `HistoryDto.coverage` (`HistoryCoverageDto`) beantwortet, was die Seite bisher verschwieg — ab wann es überhaupt Daten gibt und wie viel des Zeitraums wirklich gemessen ist. Es ist eine **EIGENE billige Abfrage** (`HistoryRepository.coverage`: `min/max/count` + `lag()` auf `telemetry_rollup_15m`, RLS-gefenced wie alles hier), bewusst NICHT aus dem Datenblock abgeleitet — sie bleibt damit unabhängig von der Reihe UND von der Preisreihe (`PriceSlots`), berührt deren Equi-Join also nicht. Gezählt werden **Viertelstunden** (`resolutionMinutes`), nicht Anzeige-Buckets: so entsteht die Messreihe, egal ob die Seite Tage oder Stunden zeichnet.
  Die Arithmetik ist die reine, immer laufende `HistoryService.coverage(row, window, now)`: **erwartet wird erst ab der ersten je gemessenen Viertelstunde** (ein Kalenderjahr einer im Juni ans Netz gegangenen Anlage ist nicht zu 45 % lückenhaft — es gab sie vorher nicht; genau dafür reist `firstDataAt` mit), das Ende liegt eine Rollup-Kadenz (15 min) **vor jetzt** (eine gerade beendete Viertelstunde ist keine Lücke, sondern noch nicht verdichtet), `measured` wird auf `expected` gedeckelt (nie über 100 %), und `gaps` zählt innere PLUS Rand-Fehlstellen. **Kein Prozentsatz im Vertrag** — die Zähler sind die Tatsache, die Formulierung (inkl. „nie auf 100 % aufrunden") gehört der Oberfläche. `null`, wenn die Anlage nie eine Viertelstunde gemessen hat; dokumentierte Grenze: gezählt wird die v1-Messreihe, eine rein v2-native Anlage liefert deshalb keine Abdeckung statt einer behaupteten (eine migrierte ist nicht betroffen — der Fan-out des Writers speist beide Reihen).
- **Die Ereignis-Spur des Verlaufs (F6, additives `HistoryDto.events`) — was den Ausreißer ERKLÄRT, in JEDEM Zeitraum.** Das `Tagesprotokoll` bleibt unverändert die Liste der GEWÖHNLICHEN Ereignisse eines Tages; die neue Spur (`history/Ereignisse.java`, rein wie `Tagesprotokoll`) trägt ausschließlich das Auffällige: `negativpreis` (Preisreihe unter null), `abregelung` (GEPLANT aus `schedule.curtail_kw` — der Text sagt „eingeplant"), `netzgrenze` (§ 14a aus `telemetry.grid_limit_kw`), `netzladen` (gemessen), `datenluecke` und `geraet-still`. Der Vertrag ist bewusst schmal (`HistoryEventDto`: type/start/end/text): **`text` ist die Aussage OHNE Zeitangabe** — die Zeit steht in `start`/`end` und wird von der Oberfläche zeitraumgerecht davorgesetzt. Fenster entstehen durch Verschmelzen aufeinanderfolgender Viertelstunden; je Art werden höchstens `MAX_PER_TYPE` (20) ausgeliefert, die LÄNGSTEN zuerst.
  Vier schmale, additive Abfragen liefern je Viertelstunde EINE Tatsache (`HistoryRepository.negativePriceSlots`/`curtailSlots`/`gridLimitSlots`/`gridChargeSlots` + `dataGaps`, alle RLS-gefenced wie der Rest); Fenster, Texte und Obergrenzen rechnet die reine Klasse — dasselbe Muster wie `CoverageRow`. Drei Entscheidungen, die man kennen muss:
  - **§ 14a wird nur für Tag und Woche ausgewertet** (`Ereignisse.evaluatesGridLimit`): `grid_limit_kw` steht NUR in der rohen `telemetry`, die Rollup-Kaskade führt die Spalte nicht — ein Jahresfenster wäre ein Scan über Millionen Rohzeilen. **Die Oberfläche muss das aussprechen**, sonst läse sich das Fehlen eines Markers als „keine Netzgrenze"; der Zwilling ist `frontend/portal/src/historieEreignisse.ts` `spurHinweis` — beide zusammen ändern.
  - **Netzladen wird nur behauptet, wo es bewiesen ist:** die Viertelstunde muss PV UND Ladung UND Netzbezug gemessen haben (`pv_kwh IS NOT NULL`), sonst wäre jede Ladung einer PV-losen Messreihe „aus dem Netz". Der genannte Wert ist die vorsichtigere der beiden Schranken (`least(charge − pv, import)`).
  - **Die Lücken-Marker stimmen mit der Zeit-Leiste überein:** `HistoryService` holt die Abdeckungs-Rohzahlen EINMAL und liest sie zweimal — die Zeit-Leiste zählt daraus die Fehlstellen, die Spur setzt ihre Rand-Marker auf dieselben Grenzen. Ein Schluss-Rand ist `geraet-still` statt `datenluecke`, wenn die Anlage auch NACH diesem Zeitraum nichts mehr gemessen hat (`lastInWindow == siteLast`) — der Unterschied zwischen „damals fehlte etwas" und „es kommt gerade nichts".
- **Tests**: `TagesprotokollTest` + `HistoryRangeTest` + **`HistoryCoverageTest`** + **`EreignisseTest`** (pure unit, always run: heuristics on synthetic days, Berlin/DST window arithmetic, totals formulas + null semantics, die Abdeckungs-Rechnung inkl. „erst ab der ersten Messung", laufender Viertelstunde, Rand-Lücken, Deckelung — und die Spur: Fenster-Verschmelzung, die vier Texte, Innen-/Rand-Lücken vs. stilles Gerät, „ohne Abdeckung keine Lücke, Markt-Ereignisse bleiben", Obergrenze + Chronologie); `PortalApiTest.historyDayComputesTotalsProtocolAndPlanOverlay` + `historyWeekAggregatesFromTheRollupTables` + **`historyCarriesTheDataCoverageOfThePeriod`** (real TimescaleDB + Keycloak: fixed past-day seed with hand-computed expectations, protocol events, plan overlay, the rollup path incl. `CALL refresh_telemetry_rollups`, RLS 404, bad range 400).

## Shadow-mode forecasting (model registry + Prognosequalität)

The captain-approved ML path with **Nachvollziehbarkeit as the core requirement** (full lifecycle doc: [`docs/forecasting.md`](docs/forecasting.md)).
The decision layer stays the exact MILP; the forecast layer is a measurable, comparable, swappable **model registry**: an ML challenger must PROVABLY beat the baseline on recorded history before it may ever influence a plan - and the evidence is visible in the portal in plain German.

- **Registry + tagging** (`voltpilot_forecast/registry.py`): every forecaster carries a stable model id; every persisted `forecast` row carries it in the `model` column (PK widened to include it). Baselines `load-persistence` (`SeasonalPersistenceLoadForecaster`) and `pv-physical` (`PhysicalPvForecaster`) stay the ACTIVE defaults. Challengers `load-xgb` and `pv-residual-xgb` run in **shadow**: they predict + persist every cycle, nothing downstream consumes them.
- **Active model = env, promotion = a human flip.** `VOLTPILOT_ACTIVE_LOAD_MODEL` / `VOLTPILOT_ACTIVE_PV_MODEL` (defaults = baselines) feed three consumers that must stay in sync: the **optimizer** (`inputs.py` filters its forecast reads on the active model - proven by `tests/test_active_model.py`), the **forecast collector** (logs them) and the **api** (renders the "live" badge). There is NO automatic promotion; the captain flips the env after the evaluation proves a sustained win, and can flip back the same way.
- **The challengers** (`voltpilot_forecast/ml.py`, optional `[ml]` extra = xgboost+numpy via the native Booster API - no sklearn; LightGBM's wheels were no friendlier, both need OpenMP: macOS `brew install libomp`, the Dockerfile installs libgomp1): `load-xgb` = XGBoost on tabular features (`features.py`: Berlin-local slot-of-day/weekday/weekend + German-holiday flag (`holidays.py`, Gauss Easter), leakage-safe lags -1d/-7d, windowed means, outdoor temperature from stored weather); `pv-residual-xgb` = the physical PV model + a learned residual on weather features (prediction = physical + residual, clipped to [0, capacity]). Deterministic seeds.
- **Self-gating (honesty)**: a challenger refuses to train below **21 full telemetry days** (`VOLTPILOT_ML_MIN_DAYS`; a day counts at >= 48 of 96 slots) - below the gate it emits NO predictions and records `status='collecting'` + days_collected/required in `forecast_model_state`, which the portal renders as "Sammelt Daten: Tag X von 21". Nightly retrain (first collector cycle per Berlin day) persists the explainability trail: trained_at, train_rows, top-5 feature importances with plain-German labels.
- **Collector** (`forecast_collect.py`, console script `voltpilot-forecast-collect`, compose `feeds` profile as **`forecast-collector`**, prod service `forecast`): every 15 min per site it loads telemetry + stored weather (latest run per hour - past hours = "the weather that was forecast then"), runs baselines + gated challengers, persists all model-tagged, upserts `forecast_model_state`, and once per Berlin day triggers the evaluation - one container for collect + retrain + evaluate. PV nameplate is estimated from the observed generation peak (+5 %) until plants are modelled.
- **Evaluation** (`evaluation.py` pure math + `evaluate.py` DB glue, `voltpilot-forecast-eval`): per site x model x **Berlin day**, compares each model's per-slot FRESHEST prediction issued at/before the slot (`run_at <= time` - what the optimizer would have consumed) against 15-min telemetry actuals -> `forecast_accuracy` (MAE kW, nMAE % - NULL on all-zero days, bias, **skill = 1 - mae/mae_baseline**, NULL for the baseline itself). Plan economics -> `plan_accuracy` (freshest ex-ante plan cost + no-battery baseline vs realized signed grid cost from telemetry x price, over exactly the comparable slots). All upserts idempotent - re-running a day after late telemetry overwrites.
- **Schema** (api migration **`V20260701040000`**, canonical runtime owner): retrofits/creates `forecast.model` (backfills old rows as the baseline ids, widens the PK) and owns `forecast_model_state`, `forecast_accuracy`, `plan_accuracy` - all three tenant-stamped + RLS-scoped exactly like telemetry (writers = trusted backend role, api reads via the app role). `forecast` itself stays without RLS (backend-only consumers). Service-side mirror migration `services/forecast/migrations/V20260702000000__forecast_model_column.sql`; bootstrap mirrors in `infra/local/timescale/02-forecast.sql` + `infra/prod/timescale/00-collector-tables.sql`.
- **API + portal**: `GET /api/v1/sites/{siteId}/forecast-quality` (SiteController; active models from config, model states, daily error/skill series, plan economics; foreign site 404, fresh site = empty well-formed lists; in `openapi.yaml`) feeds the portal page **"Prognosequalität"** (`pages/PrognosePage.tsx`, nav id `prognose`): live model per Verbrauch/PV with Ø-Abweichung (7 Tage), challenger cards (honest "Sammelt Daten: Tag X von 21" progress vs "In X der letzten Y Bewertungen genauer"), per-kind MAE comparison chart (`ForecastQualityChart.tsx`: active solid blue, challenger dashed orange), top-feature list in plain German, and the shadow-mode explainer ("keinerlei Einfluss ... automatisch passiert das nie").
- **Tests (the assurance)**: forecast - `test_registry.py` (tagging + env resolution + per-model repository isolation), `test_holidays.py`/`test_features.py` (computus; leakage-free lags; every feature has a German label), `test_ml.py` (gating below/at threshold, deterministic seed, **skill sanity: the learner beats persistence on a weekly pattern with skill > 0.5** and the PV residual corrects a systematic bias), `test_evaluation.py` (exact hand-computed MAE/nMAE/skill/plan-economics, DST day bounds, idempotent upserts), `test_collect.py` (full collector cycle over a fake DB + evaluate_day end to end); optimization - `test_active_model.py` (only active-model rows consumed; env flip switches). Api: `PortalApiTest.forecastQualityIsTenantScopedAndMarksTheActiveModel` (real TimescaleDB+Keycloak, RLS 404). CI runs the forecast matrix with `.[dev,ml]` so the challenger tests run in the gate.

## MaStR integration ("Anlage verknüpfen": registry-fed asset master data)

The OPTIONAL site-level onboarding step: the customer enters their **SEE unit number(s)** (PV + optionally storage; storage units are ALSO SEE-numbered) from the BNetzA registration confirmation, the api fetches the public Marktstammdatenregister record, the portal shows it in plain German for CONFIRMATION, and only "Übernehmen" persists the values onto the site's assets - feeding the PV forecast (PlantSpec) and the optimizer's battery params with authoritative values instead of assumptions.
Registry data is a customer-confirmed prefill, NOT ground truth.
Groundwork: the feasibility scout report (access paths, verified field availability, masking rules) and the captain's proven energy-sharing-voltpilot SOAP implementation, which `MastrSoapClient` ports.

- **Dual-source port** (`services/api` `mastr/` package, the DayAheadPriceSource pattern): `PlantRegistryClient` is the anti-corruption interface; `MastrJsonClient` (keyless public web-search JSON backend, exact unit-number Kendo filter) is the DEFAULT so dev + a fresh deployment need no secret; `MastrSoapClient` (official BNetzA webservice) activates when BOTH `MASTR_API_KEY` + `MASTR_MARKTAKTEUR_NUMMER` are set - promotion is pure config. The captain's one-time free registration (MaStR account -> Marktakteur -> Webdienstnutzer) yields the credentials; blank placeholders live in `.env.example`/`.env.prod.example`, never a real key.
- **The SOAP envelope is built by string, deliberately** (ported from the proven reference; live-verified 2026-07-02): generic SOAP libs mishandle the WCF namespace split - the wrapper element needs the `Request` suffix in the `Modelle/Anlage` namespace while `apiKey`/`marktakteurMastrNummer`/`einheitMastrNummer` sit in the `Modelle` namespace, and `SOAPAction` is the BARE method name in quotes. A SEE lookup tries `GetEinheitSolar` then `GetEinheitStromSpeicher` (method map extensible for SEW/SEB/... later). The JSON backend needs two hard guards (it silently ignores misspelled filter columns): `Total == 1` AND the returned `MaStRNummer` must echo the request.
- **Catalog decoding** (`MastrCatalog`): the registry stores orientation/tilt/battery technology as catalog IDS. Azimuth 695..702 -> 0..315° (PlantSpec convention, 180 = Süd); tilt bins 806..810 -> midpoints (12.5/30/50/75/90°); **Ost-West (704) and nachgeführt (703/811) map to NO single value** - degrees stay null (forecaster defaults apply), the label is shown honestly, a German warning explains. Balkonkraftwerke carry no orientation at all -> nulls, no warning ("nicht im Register hinterlegt" in the UI). Location for private operators is masked to PLZ+Ort - shown for the customer's own plausibility check, NEVER written to site coordinates.
- **Endpoints** (`MastrController`, tenant-scoped like all site routes, RLS 404 for foreign sites; in `openapi.yaml`, tag `registry`): `POST /api/v1/sites/{id}/mastr-lookup {einheitNummer}` -> mapped preview, nothing persisted (error bodies carry a customer-facing German `message`: wrong-prefix hints incl. SES/SSE/EEG, not-found, registry-down 502); `POST /api/v1/sites/{id}/mastr-apply {pv?, storage?}` -> upserts the site's `pv`/`battery` asset rows (update-in-place keeps row ids + device links; battery values land in the EXISTING optimizer columns, `roundtrip_efficiency_pct` untouched) + provenance; `GET /api/v1/sites/{id}/assets` -> rows incl. provenance for the drawer.
- **Schema** (api migration **`V20260702010000`** - `...02000000` is taken by services/forecast): asset gains nullable `pv_capacity_kwp`, `module_count`, `azimuth_deg`, `tilt_deg`, `commissioned_on` + registry-NEUTRAL provenance (`registry`, `registry_unit_id`, `registry_fetched_at` - an AT/CH registry is a drop-in later). Existing asset RLS policy + grants (V2) cover the new columns; no infra bootstrap mirror needed (pure ALTER ... ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS layers over any volume). Migration **`V20260703010000`** then adds a **unique index on `asset (site_id, type)`** so the apply is an atomic `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT (site_id, type) DO UPDATE` (`AssetRepository`, wrapped in `@Transactional` on `MastrController.apply`) that can never double-insert on a concurrent re-submit - this formalizes the long-standing "one asset row per (site, type)" invariant, so the deferred **multi-battery-sites** future work must drop/rework this constraint.
- **Forecast wiring**: `forecast_collect.load_sites` LEFT JOINs the site's `pv` asset; a linked site forecasts with `PlantSpec(pv_capacity_kwp, azimuth_deg|180, tilt_deg|30)` instead of the observed-peak estimate (which stays the fallback for unlinked sites). The optimizer needed NO change - applied storage values land in the asset columns `inputs.py` already reads.
- **Portal** (`components/MastrDrawer.tsx` + the "Anlage" section in the `StandortePage` detail drawer): input step (client-side `^SEE\d{12}$` validation with the same friendly SES/SSE/EEG hints) -> confirmation cards (plain German, nullable fields as "nicht im Register hinterlegt", warnings, masked-location note, DL-DE-BY-2.0 attribution) -> Übernehmen -> linked state shows kWp/orientation/storage + MaStR numbers + "zuletzt abgerufen" + a "MaStR verknüpft" badge. The device-claim flow is untouched.
- **Tests**: `MastrSoapClientTest` (exact envelope/SOAPAction shape, fixture parsing, solar->storage fallthrough, fault mapping), `MastrJsonClientTest` (VERBATIM-recorded public-backend fixtures of the two real sample units SEE966831669444/SEE972142227037 in `src/test/resources/mastr/` - no live registry call in CI, provenance in the fixtures README), `MastrCatalogTest` (every catalog id from the report's tables), `MastrNumbersTest`/`MastrServiceTest`, and `MastrApiTest` (Testcontainers TimescaleDB+Keycloak; the REAL `MastrJsonClient` runs against a fixture-serving `MastrHttp` bean via `ObjectProvider` - lookup preview, apply persists + provenance, RLS foreign-site 404 on all three routes). Forecast: `test_collect.py` proves the linked PlantSpec is preferred over the peak estimate and `load_sites` builds it from the joined asset row.

## Self-maintaining battery-asset <-> device link + manual battery editor

Captain design 2026-07-07: the battery is controlled by the inverter, the inverter is the edge device, so `asset.device_id` (what `services/optimization` `publisher.py` publishes the plan against) must maintain ITSELF.
Before this, MaStR-created (and dev-seed) battery assets left `device_id` NULL, so the optimizer PERSISTED a plan but NEVER published it - the edge showed "noch kein Fahrplan empfangen" while the portal showed a plan (a real customer plant hit exactly this).

- **The rule (one place):** `AssetRepository.autoLinkBatteryDevice(siteId)` links a site's UNLINKED battery asset to its device WHEN the site has EXACTLY ONE device (`(array_agg(id))[1]` - Postgres has no `max(uuid)`; `HAVING count(*)=1` makes the pick unambiguous). A multi-device site is left alone on purpose - never guess which inverter controls the battery; already-linked batteries are never re-pointed. RLS-scoped (only the caller's tenant).
- **Three layers, same rule:** (1) BACKFILL migration `V20260707010000` (runs as the Flyway superuser, heals all tenants on deploy incl. the captain's live plant); (2) ON CLAIM - `DeviceController.claim` calls it after a fresh claim (a claim can make a site single-device); (3) ON WRITE - `MastrController.apply` (after `applyBattery`) and the manual battery editor call it.
- **Manual battery editor (also closes the long-standing "non-MaStR plants can't maintain battery data" gap):** `PUT /api/v1/sites/{siteId}/battery` (`SiteBatteryController`, `SaveBatteryRequest`) upserts capacity/charge/discharge/`roundtrip_efficiency_pct` (leaves registry provenance untouched, so a manual battery keeps a NULL registry) and maintains the link: an explicit `deviceId` (validated to belong to the site, else 404) picks the controlling device on a multi-device site; omitting it runs the auto-link. Tenant-scoped like every site route (foreign site 404; admins via the `X-Tenant-Id` switcher).
- **Speicherschonung customer preset (FK4, captain decision from the vp-solver-xlsx-f2 audit):** the same battery PUT takes an optional `speicherschonung` enum (`aggressiv|ausgewogen|schonend`) mapped server-side by the ONE constant `web/Speicherschonung.java` onto `asset.wear_cost_ct_per_kwh` (1/4/8 ct; values sit safely inside the report's dispatch-invariance band - tipping point ~12 ct) and touching ONLY that column, never the admin SoC-band/backup-reserve overrides; null/absent keeps the stored value, and the optimizer needs NO change (inputs.py reads the column per 15-min cycle). Read side: `SiteAssetDto.speicherschonung` is the derived EFFECTIVE preset (NULL wear→`ausgewogen` since it equals the 4-ct platform default; off-ladder admin value→`individuell`, shown as "Individuell (durch VoltPilot konfiguriert)" and overwritten when the customer picks a preset - the raw ct value stays admin-only in optimizer-config). Portal: three-option radio "Umgang mit dem Speicher" in the `BatteryEditForm` + a read-first row, copy WITHOUT ct/kWh numbers, pure helpers in `src/speicherschonung.ts` (the edit form sends the field only when the choice actually changed, so an untouched save never pins/overwrites). Proven by `PortalApiTest.speicherschonungPresetsWriteExactlyTheWearColumnAndDeriveHonestly` + portal `speicherschonung.test.ts`/`AnlageTechnik.test.tsx`.
- **Visible warning, not silent failure:** `OverviewSiteDto.batteryWithoutDevice` (a battery asset with `device_id` NULL - `OverviewRepository.sitesWithUnlinkedBattery()`) drives the plain-German warning (`fleet.ts` `BATTERY_NO_DEVICE_WARNING`/`_SHORT`): an amber "Speicher ohne Gerät" badge on the fleet site card, the same alert in the single-site Übersicht status card, and the full "Speicher & Steuerung" section in the `StandortePage` detail drawer (params table + controlling device + editor + warning, sourced from ALL assets not just registry-linked). `SiteAssetDto` gained `deviceId` + `roundtripEfficiencyPct`.
- **Optimizer is UNCHANGED** - it already keys publish off `asset.device_id`; the api's auto-link is what heals a site (`test_engine.py::test_autolink_makes_a_previously_unpublished_site_publish`).
- **Tests:** `PortalApiTest` (`batteryAssetAutoLinksToTheSitesSingleDeviceAndNeverGuessesOnMultiDeviceSites` - on-claim + on-write + multi-device no-guess + explicit picker + overview flag; `batteryEditorIsTenantScopedAndValidatesTheChosenDevice`; `backfillLinksSingleDeviceSitesButLeavesMultiDeviceSitesUnlinked` - the migration SQL in isolation via superuser); portal `StandortePage.test.tsx` (`BatteryControlSection` warning/linked/add/save) + `fleet.test.ts` (warning copy).

## Inverter control: physical execution + register-level readback (`vp-inverter-control`)

Captain-approved tranche (2026-07-08, decisions in firstmate `vp-inverter-control`): turn the Fahrplan from "displayed/simulated" into "physically executed, with register-level readback proof".
The write side MIRRORS the read self-wiring; adding a control family = one adapter, no core change.
**SAFETY IS NON-NEGOTIABLE (captain decision 3): control is OFF by default and no model writes live until bench-certified.**
The automated proof is against the SIMULATOR only (`edge-app/test/e2e-compose.sh`, which now asserts write→readback→match on reg 40/41/42); real Deye writes ship UNCERTIFIED (read-only) until the captain's bench session.

- **The control-adapter abstraction (`edge-app/nodered/inverter-control-routing.js`, the write twin of `inverter-routing.js`).** Pure `controlRoute(selection, setpoint, opts) -> {adapter, family, certified, controlEnabled, writes[], readbacks[], planned?, reason?}`; a separate executor does the I/O. `WriteOp = {role, fc, addr, value, encode, dwell_s, min_change}`, `ReadOp = {role, fc:3, addr, expect, tolerance}`. **Two independent gates:** (1) `controlEnabled` = the CORE kill-switch AND per-model cert verdict, published on `edge/setpoint`; when false `writes` is EMPTY but `readbacks` still run (UI shows actual state). (2) `CERTIFIED_CONTROL_FAMILIES = {sunspec}` HERE - an uncertified family (every Deye family) emits `writes:[]`/`readbacks:[]` REGARDLESS of the kill-switch, its intended ToU mapping surfaced as `planned` (display/tests only, never executed). Signs are config (`invert_control_sign`), never code. The flow's "Steuerung / Schreibplan" + "schreiben + zuruecklesen" function nodes carry a SYNCED COPY (like the read side), pinned by `flows-sync.test.js`; `build-flows.js` authors both the self-wiring auto tab's control path AND the Simulator tab's (fixed `edge-sim` sunspec selection).
- **generic_modbus/SunSpec control adapter (CERTIFIED, proven vs sim):** `battery_power`→FC6 reg40 (int16 kW×100), `control_enable`→reg41=1, `pv_limit`→reg42 (uint16 kW×100, `0xFFFF` sentinel). After each write, FC3-reads 40/41/42 back, computes per-register `{commanded, actual, match}`, publishes `edge/control/readback` (NEW local-bus topic, NOT retained). Modbus FC6 write PDUs added to `modbus-tcp.js` (`buildWriteSingleRequest`/`parseWriteSingleResponse`), exercised end-to-end against an in-process server in `modbus-tcp.e2e.test.js` (write→readback→match + mismatch case + Deye read-only no-op).
- **Deye control adapter (UNCERTIFIED, read-only until bench).** `DEYE_CONTROL_REG` in `inverter-control-routing.js` is now a **per-battery-family map SOURCED from ha-solarman** (`davidrapan/ha-solarman`, MIT: `deye_p3.yaml`→`hybrid_3p` = SG04LP3 LV **and** SG01HP3 HV; `deye_hybrid.yaml`→`hybrid_1p` = SG0\*LP1), NOT triangulated - ha-solarman drives the SAME Solarman-V5 logger, so the addresses are belastbar; only the register facts are re-expressed, no GPL/MIT code copied (attribution in-code + DEYE.md). **The earlier 0x0F00-region guess was WRONG** (a read-only bench dump of the captain's SG04LP3 showed 0x0F00.. holds LIVE TELEMETRY, not config - data/learnings.md 2026-07-08); the real levers live OUTSIDE the telemetry window in `0x008D..0x00B1` (3p) / `0x00F3..0x0117` (1p). Mapping (strategy A, ONE live ToU Program-1 slot): `work_mode`→Work Mode reg (0x008E/0x00F4 = Export First), `tou_enable`→ToU reg (0x0092/0x00F8 = 0x00FF all-week), `battery_power`→Program 1 Power (0x009A/0x0100, W/`power_scale`), `battery_target_soc`→Program 1 SOC (0x00A6/0x010C, direction via target-SoC), `grid_charge_enable`→Program 1 Charging enum (0x00AC/0x0112, Disabled/Grid, EEG-gated by `netzladen_erlaubt`), `pv_limit`→the family feed-in cap (3p "Grid Max Export power" 0x00E7 scale 10 / 1p "Max Sell Power" 0x00F5 scale 1; OMITTED when uncurtailed = contract "absent = no limit"). String/micro (no battery) plan only `pv_limit`→active-power-limit 0x0028 (the correct reg THERE; it was wrong on hybrids and removed). All `bench_pending`, never executed. Write path = standard Modbus FC6/FC16 in the V5 frame (`deye/solarman-v5.js` `writeSingleRegisterRequest`/`writeMultipleRegistersRequest`) - byte-identical to ha-solarman's `WRITE_SINGLE_REGISTER`/`WRITE_MULTIPLE_REGISTERS`, so no frame change was needed. **Bench checklist (what the captain must verify per model before certifying): [`edge-app/nodered/CONTROL-BENCH.md`](edge-app/nodered/CONTROL-BENCH.md)** (ha-solarman base addresses, direction-via-target-SoC, sign, current-vs-power, readback fidelity, EEPROM cadence, fail-safe). `inverter-control-routing.test.js` pins the per-family registers + the "no control reg inside the telemetry block" invariant; `flows-sync.test.js` guards the inline flow copy for both hybrid + string branches.
- **`vp-control-readback` vp-palette node** (publisher to `edge/control/readback`, the write twin of `vp-status`; NOT retained). New `edge/setpoint` fields (all backward-compatible): `pv_limit_kw` (guard-clamped `[0,∞)`, ABSENT clears any latch - the core now forwards curtailment via `plan.ActivePvLimit`), `control_enabled`, `grid_charge_allowed`, `soc_min_pct`.
- **Core (Go, `edge-app/core`):** config `VP_CONTROL_ENABLED` (default **false**) + `VP_CONTROL_CERTIFIED_FAMILIES` (default `sunspec`; bench adds e.g. `hybrid_3p`) + `VP_GRID_CHARGE_ALLOWED` (default false = EEG-safe); `Config.ControlCertified(family)` (empty family = certified, the dev/sim path). `applySetpoint` publishes the gate + pv_limit; the agent subscribes `edge/control/readback` into `Snapshot.Control` (+ `ControlEnabled`/`ControlCertified`) and folds a compact `control` block into the status heartbeat (`cloud.ControlSummary` - additive, schema_version stays "1.0"). The `:8484` **"Steuerung & Bestätigung"** card (`static/control.js` `window.VPControl.onState`, //go:embed - rebuild core after edits) shows per register role+address, commanded (kW+raw), read-back actual, accept/mismatch + a mismatch banner; honest states for uncertified/off/waiting.
- **Portal (api + frontend):** the status listener has a sibling `ControlStatusListener` (same `ems/+/+/+/status` filter, parses the `control` block; `VOLTPILOT_CONTROL_MQTT_LISTENER_ENABLED`, on in both composes) that upserts a RLS-scoped `device_control_status` row (api migration `V20260708000000`, one row/device, tenant-stamped). `GET /api/v1/sites/{siteId}/control-status` (SiteController, RLS 404 foreign, 204 until a readback) → `ControlStatusRepository.latestForSite`. The Anlagen-Seite gets a calm **"Steuerung" strip** (`components/ControlStrip.tsx` fed by the pure `src/control.ts` `controlStrip()` → `healthy|mismatch|stale|off|pending`, plain German, NO register/Modbus jargon - that lives on the technician's :8484 card). Tests: `PortalApiTest.controlStatusIsIngestedFromHeartbeatAndTenantScoped` (listener handle() incl. spoofed-identity skip + RLS 404 + upsert newest-wins), portal `control.test.ts`.
- **Safety invariants (report §6.1, all enforced):** guards stay authoritative upstream (the adapter never widens the clamped kW); kill-switch off by default; per-model certified allowlist (uncertified → read-only, UI "noch nicht freigegeben"); fail-safe neutral on stale/loss; EEG grid-charge default off. **Never ship a guessed register address as certified** - the Deye ToU addresses are `bench_pending` and produce no live write.
- **Fronius SunSpec curtailment (Increment 1, `vp-fronius-control-curtail`, UNCERTIFIED/planned-only).** Fronius control uses standards-based **SunSpec Modbus** (design report `data/vp-fronius-control-scout-c4`; NOT the Solar-API read path, NOT the rejected evcc `config/timeofuse` HTTP hack). NEW capability: a **real SunSpec model-discovery walker** [`edge-app/nodered/sunspec/model-discovery.js`](edge-app/nodered/sunspec/model-discovery.js) - walks the dynamic model linked list from the SID base (40001/"SunS"), locates Common(1)/Nameplate(120)/**Immediate Controls(123)**/Storage(124), handles int+SF (101/102/103) AND float (111/112/113); **addresses are DISCOVERED LIVE, never hard-coded** (field offsets within a model are the fixed SunSpec definition; the model BASE is discovered), pure/offline-tested (`model-discovery.test.js`). This replaces the sim-only 9-register `sunspec` PROFILE in `modbus-tcp.js` (that is NOT real SunSpec). `planCurtailment` maps `pv_limit_kw` → Model 123 `WMaxLimPct` (kW → % of discovered nameplate `WRtg`) + `WMaxLim_Ena` + `WMaxLimPct_RvrtTms` (the vendor dead-man's-switch), value+revert-timer written BEFORE the enable, uncurtailed slot DISABLES the limit; idle-safe (no writes, no fabricated address) if the SID/Model 123 is missing. Wired into `inverter-control-routing.js` `froniusControl` as adapter **`fronius_sunspec`** (≠ `modbus_tcp`, so the flow write/readback executor no-ops on it, like Deye's `solarman_v5`): Fronius is DELIBERATELY absent from `CERTIFIED_CONTROL_FAMILIES`/`VP_CONTROL_CERTIFIED_FAMILIES`, so `writes:[]`/`readbacks:[]` always, the intended plan is `planned`/`bench_pending` only (needs `opts.sunspec` discovery, absent in-flow this increment → empty planned). Control endpoint is the separate Modbus surface (`connection.control_port` default 502, `control_unit_id` default 1; same ip as the port-80 read). Signs/scaling VERIFY-on-device. Going live needs a real-hardware bench pass (FRONIUS.md §6 + CONTROL-BENCH.md Fronius).
- **Fronius SunSpec battery charge/discharge (Increment 2, `vp-fronius-control-battery`, UNCERTIFIED/planned-only).** Extends the SAME discovery walker + `froniusControl` (report §1.3/§3.2/§3.4) with **Model 124 (Storage)**: `planStorage` (in `model-discovery.js`) maps `battery_setpoint_kw` (+charge/−discharge) → `InWRte`/`OutWRte` as **% of the discovered `WChaMax`** (nameplate max charge rate, read live w/ `WChaMax_SF`; the `InOutWRte_SF`-scaled register value), picking the direction register + the `StorCtl_Mod` bit (bit0 charge / bit1 discharge; **0 = release/self-consumption** for an idle 0-kW setpoint — no Deye-style target-SoC hack, the rate registers carry direction directly), plus `MinRsvPct` from `soc_min` (reserve floor), the EEG-gated `ChaGriSet` grid-charge gate (`GRID` only when `grid_charge_allowed` AND charging, else `PV`/off — default off, from `site.netzladen_erlaubt`), and the native `InOutWRte_RvrtTms` dead-man's-switch. Ordering is safety-load-bearing: rates + reserve + grid-gate + revert-timer written BEFORE the `StorCtl_Mod` enable bits (armed LAST). `froniusControl` merges curtailment (Inc 1) + storage `planned` lists; `invert_control_sign` flips direction (config, never code). **Higher risk** — a wrong sign/scale can affect a real battery's health/warranty — so like Inc 1 Fronius stays ABSENT from `CERTIFIED_CONTROL_FAMILIES`/`VP_CONTROL_CERTIFIED_FAMILIES`: `writes:[]`/`readbacks:[]` ALWAYS, storage plan is `planned`/`bench_pending` only (needs `opts.sunspec` discovery; absent in-flow → empty planned, so `flows.json`/`flows-sync.test.js` stay green — the flow does no in-flow discovery). Idle-safe if Model 124 is absent (batteryless inverter → curtailment-only) or WChaMax unknown. Battery control is **per-battery-brand** bench_pending (BYD/LG/Fronius) like Deye. No Go/core change (the core already publishes `battery_setpoint_kw`/`soc_min`/`grid_charge_allowed`/`control_enabled` on `edge/setpoint`; the flow executor no-ops on `fronius_sunspec`). Going live needs a real-hardware bench pass per brand (CONTROL-BENCH.md → Fronius Storage checklist, FRONIUS.md §6). Model 802/803 battery-bank detail = calibration cross-check only, not written.

## Multi-source Anlage (Phase 1): N Erzeuger per site, aggregated ON THE EDGE

The fix for the general "AC-coupled PV invisible -> garbage load (46,7 kW)" bug: a site with a **battery-hybrid inverter PLUS a separate AC-coupled PV** was measured wrong because the edge read only the one inverter, so the hybrid's balance misattributed the second PV's ~70 kW to load. Phase 1 lets a site have **N additional read-only Erzeuger (PV) measurement points** and **sums them into ONE composite site reading on the EDGE**, so the existing single `edge/telemetry` contract stays byte-for-byte unchanged downstream (ingest/rollups/portal/earnings/optimizer untouched - the rollups `avg()` per site, which is exactly why aggregation must NOT happen in the cloud). Design: `data/vp-multisource-edge-design/report.md` §3/§4 (the Luxone role-tagged measurement-point tree; Phase 1 = the **Erzeuger** role, then **Increment 1** adds the **Netz** grid-meter role — see the Increment 1 subsection below; Verbraucher/Wallbox still deferred). Captain decisions 2026-07-09.

- **Edge core aggregation (`edge-app/core/internal/sources` + `agent`).** `sources.Source` reuses the inverter `Catalog` (brand/model/family/communication/connection) + `role`/`capacity_kwp`/`registry_unit_id`/`interval_s`; persisted `sources.json` (like `inverter.json`) and re-published **retained** on the NEW local-bus topic `edge/sources/config` (`sources.BusConfig`). A source is **READ-ONLY BY CONSTRUCTION** (no control field/topic ever). Additional sources publish per-source telemetry on `edge/sources/{id}/telemetry`; the agent subscribes the wildcard, keeps the latest PV per source with a freshness window (`max(3*interval, 60s)` - **error isolation: a dead/stale source is ABSENT, never a fabricated 0**), and at the existing `onLocalTelemetry` choke point sets `site_pv = primary_pv + Σ fresh Erzeuger` BEFORE the guards run; the house then follows the **captain-decreed standard (2026-07-17)** `house = pv_total + grid − battery` (see the house-consumption-standard note in edge-app/AGENTS.md) - the old `max(0, load - Σ Erzeuger)` netting estimate survives only on the raw-load fallback path (no usable site grid, or an unknown-family primary without a measured battery). **Zero sources = byte-for-byte the single-source path** (empty cache, no publishers on the wildcard). The physical envelope PV bound widens to Σ generation nameplate (`guards.EnvelopePvBound`; primary rating + Σ `capacity_kwp`), battery bound stays the primary's. Web API `GET/POST/DELETE /api/sources` (`web.SourcesController`; add form reuses `GET /api/inverter` catalog). The `:8484` inverter page gained a "Weitere Energiequellen" list+form (`static/sources.js`; `//go:embed` - rebuild core after edits). Tests: `internal/sources` + `agent/source_agg_test.go` (two-source sum + load correction, absent/stale isolation, zero-source byte-for-byte, envelope widen/narrow) + `web`.
- **Node-RED read fan-out (`edge-app/nodered`).** `vp-sources-config` palette node (subscribe retained `edge/sources/config`, emit the array -> `flow.sources_config`) + `vp-quelle` node (publish one source reading on `edge/sources/{id}/telemetry`, topic-injection-guarded). `sources-routing.js` (`parseSourcesConfig`/`planSources`, reusing `inverter-routing.route`). `build-flows.js` "Energiequellen (automatisch)" tab: sources-config -> store (modbus read plans) -> poll -> **sequential** per-source Modbus read (error-isolated) -> vp-quelle; `flows.json` regenerated (idempotent), `flows-sync.test.js` pins the inlined store + SunSpec PV decode. **SCOPE (E6, all read transports wired): the flow executor reads EVERY read transport in EVERY source slot** - `modbus_tcp` (the compact sim profile the e2e uses), `fronius_sunspec` via the embedded SunSpec-live discovery walk (`sunspec/model-discovery.js` + `sunspec/sunspec-live.js`), `goe_http_api` (a go-e wallbox Consumer), `solarman_v5` (a Deye over its Solarman-V5 logger - `readSolarmanReading` embeds `deye/solarman-v5.js` + `deye/deye-decode.js`) and `fronius_solar_api` (a Fronius over its Solar API v1 - `readFroniusReading` embeds `fronius/solar-api.js`). The store validates the Deye family + logger serial and the Go `sources.busEntry()` forwards each transport's connection fields (solarman serial/scale, fronius insecure_tls/invert_grid_sign, SunSpec unit/model_type). Publish per role: Erzeuger -> `pv_power_kw`, Netz -> signed `power_kw`, Consumer -> `load_kw`; sequential (never two sockets to one device), error-isolated (a dead source -> absent, never a fabricated 0). The whole class of "recognised by routing but plan silently dropped" (the 2026-07-13 `sunspec_live` device-down) is now closed for the remaining transports. Proven by `sources-read.e2e.test.js` (in-process SunSpec/Solarman-V5/HTTP servers: Erzeuger publishes `pv_power_kw`, Netz publishes signed `power_kw`, Consumer publishes `load_kw`, dead source error-isolated + warned).
- **Data model (`services/api` + portal).** `asset(site_id, type)` STAYS the AGGREGATE PV/battery the optimizer + earnings read; the PV asset's kWp becomes **primary + Σ additional Erzeuger** (`AssetRepository.addPvCapacity` applies exact per-op deltas on create/delete so it never clobbers a MaStR/manual primary kWp). New **`measurement_point`** child table (api migration `V20260709000000`, RLS-scoped like `asset`): role vocabulary (Phase 1 `'pv-generation'`), per-source `capacity_kwp` + `registry_unit_id` (the source's OWN MaStR SEE #, optional - captured, never blocks adding), nullable `device_id` (additional points need NO device row/cert/claim - read through the one claimed edge). **Control safety enforced in the DB:** a CHECK forbids `control=true` on any non-`battery-hybrid` role, and a partial unique index allows AT MOST ONE control point per site. `MeasurementPointController` `GET/POST/DELETE /api/v1/sites/{id}/measurement-points` (RLS 404 foreign, non-Erzeuger 400). Portal: the Technik "Mein Gerät" card's `ErzeugerSourcesPanel` records sources (master data only, no second claim; fails soft). `openapi.yaml` + `PortalApiTest` (aggregate = Σ, delete drops it, RLS/400/404, the DB CHECK + one-control constraints) + portal `ErzeugerSourcesPanel.test.tsx`.
- **Deferred (noted precisely):** syncing the portal `measurement_point` master data down to the edge `sources.json` (today the edge `:8484` UI and the portal record sources independently); the edge's live source list + per-point health now DOES reach the portal (see Increment 2 above, #524); per-source telemetry visibility in the cloud (would need the rollups to sum-per-point). (E6 wired the remaining per-source read executors - Solarman-V5, Fronius Solar API - and the portal `consumer` role; the go-e wallbox Consumer role + edge aggregation shipped 2026-07-20.)

### Increment 2 (partial): the per-source PV breakdown reaches the portal (#524)

The 2026-07-21 PV incident's real fix: the portal showed only the COMPOSITE PV, so a multi-inverter site read "39 kW" with no way to see it is Deye 8,3 + Fronius 21,3 + Fronius WR 2 9,3 - verifying meant opening the edge's `:8484`. The edge-reported per-point list now reaches the portal. Pure added VISIBILITY: the composite telemetry/aggregate path is byte-for-byte untouched, and it rides the STATUS channel, never telemetry.

- **Edge → cloud:** the additive `sources` block on the status heartbeat (`cloud.SourcesSummary`/`SourceEntry`, built by `agent.sourcesSummary()` in `internal/agent/entities.go`, capped at 16 entries): the primary inverter (its OWN pv from `Snapshot.LastReading`, captured BEFORE the multi-source fold - never the composite) plus every configured source from the existing `SourceStatuses`/`SourceLastReadings` freshness machinery. `health` is `ok|stale|never`; an absent measurement stays absent. A site with nothing configured emits NO block; a single-inverter site emits a list of one. Proof: `agent/sources_summary_test.go`.
- **Cloud:** `SourceStatusListener` (`services/api` `sources/`, flag `VOLTPILOT_SOURCES_MQTT_LISTENER_ENABLED`, on in both composes) is the third sibling on `ems/+/+/+/status` next to the control/entity listeners - same authorization posture (broker ACL + mTLS-CN, topic==payload identity re-validated, device resolved through the RLS repository under the topic tenant) - and REPLACES the device's whole set wholesale in `device_source_status` (migration `V20260721000000`, RLS like `device_control_status`), because the heartbeat carries the COMPLETE Ist. Served by `GET /api/v1/sites/{siteId}/sources` (RLS-fenced, foreign site 404, admins via the X-Tenant-Id switcher; empty list = no device reported the block yet). Proof: `PortalApiTest.siteSourcesExposeThePartsOfTheCompositePvTenantScoped`.
- **Portal:** the live view (`pages/LiveSection.tsx`, both the v1 `LiveHero` and the adaptive branch) renders `components/PvBreakdown.tsx` under the PV figure, driven by the pure `src/pvSources.ts` (`pvBreakdown`/`breakdownLine`/`sourceLabel`/`healthTitle`; `pvSources.test.ts`). It shows ONLY with 2+ measuring devices (a single-inverter site is byte-identical to before), the shown parts always sum to what they say, a producer without a reading is NAMED in a note instead of counted as 0, and a stale part keeps its last value with a grey dot. Fetch is fail-soft - an older edge/backend simply yields no breakdown.
- **Still deferred:** per-source TELEMETRY (history/rollups per point) in the cloud, and syncing the portal `measurement_point` master data down to the edge `sources.json`.

### Increment 1: Netz-Zähler (grid meter) — edge-configured, edge-aggregated, grid-authoritative

The money-relevant correctness slice on the SAME edge-authoritative pattern (report `data/vp-ms-replan-k4/report.md` Increment 1, captain-approved 2026-07-09): a dedicated grid meter at the point of common coupling makes `site_grid` an authoritative **measurement** instead of the primary hybrid inverter's CT (grid import/export drives earnings + the optimizer). **Zero cloud change, NO migration** — a meter's value simply *becomes* `power_kw`. `measurement_point.role` already reserved `'grid-meter'`.

- **Edge core** (`internal/sources` + `internal/agent`): `sources.RoleNetz = "grid-meter"` joins `RoleErzeuger`; `Normalize` accepts both (a meter needs no `capacity_kwp`, same `Catalog` transport validation). `agent.sourceReading` carries `pv *float64` AND `grid *float64` (signed, +import/-export); `onSourceTelemetry` parses `power_kw` alongside `pv_power_kw` (either absent per role). At the `onLocalTelemetry` fold, AFTER the Erzeuger PV sum, `authoritativeGrid()` (mirrors `aggregateSourcePv`, same freshness window) overrides `measurements["power_kw"]` with the newest FRESH Netz reading; a stale/absent meter falls back to the primary CT (graceful degradation, never fabricated). The grid override lands in the cloud buffer + history ring (the state snapshot has NO grid field — assert via `hist.Recent`). `applyEnvelope` sums ONLY `RoleErzeuger` kWp, so a Netz meter adds nothing to the PV bound; the history ring's battery line is the measured register (2026-07-17 standard), so the meter's `power_kw` feeds the house balance, not a battery derivation. Zero Netz sources = byte-for-byte the pre-Increment-1 path.
- **Node-RED**: `vp-netz` palette node (grid twin of `vp-quelle`, forwards signed `power_kw`, same topic-injection guard). `sources-routing.planSources` keeps `ROLE_ERZEUGER || ROLE_NETZ` and carries `role` in the plan. `build-flows.js` "Energiequellen" tab: the sequential read decodes by role — Erzeuger → `pv_power_kw` (SunSpec reg 1) to `vp-quelle` (output 1), Netz → signed `power_kw` (SunSpec reg 0) to `vp-netz` (output 2); `flows.json` regenerated, `flows-sync.test.js` pins both inline decodes.
- **Edge `:8484` form** (`static/sources.js` + `einrichten.html`): a role picker (Erzeuger / Netz-Zähler); the Netz path hides the kWp + MaStR fields. The web `SourcesController` is role-generic (validation is in `Normalize`), so no backend change there.
- **api** (master data only): `MeasurementPointController` accepts `grid-meter`, SKIPS `addPvCapacity` for it, and enforces **0–1 Netz per site** (`MeasurementPointRepository.countByRole`; a second → 409). It ALSO accepts `consumer` (E6, 2026-07-20): no `capacity_kwp` requirement, NO `addPvCapacity` effect, NO per-site count limit (a site may have several); the DB CHECK (control only on battery-hybrid) is untouched since a source is `control=FALSE`. `openapi.yaml` updated; the portal `ErzeugerSourcesPanel` gained an "Art der Energiequelle" picker (PV-Erzeuger / Verbraucher).
- **Tests**: Go `source_agg_test.go` (fresh Netz overrides / stale falls back / no-Netz no-op / envelope unchanged) + `sources_test.go` (Normalize accepts Netz, rejects unknown) + `web_test.go` (Netz add 200, unknown role 400); Node `vp-netz` shaping + routing + flows-sync; api `PortalApiTest` (Netz accepted, no PV bump, second Netz 409, unknown role 400).

## Edge `:8484` web app: TWO pages (Betrieb / Einrichten) + Technikmodus

**Structure (concept `data/vp-edge-ux-concept/concept.html`, owner-approved 2026-07-27, branch `fm/vp-edge-ux-overhaul-h6`).** The device page has exactly two jobs: **`index.html` = Betrieb** (status line, Energiefluss, Kennzahlen, Verlauf, Fahrplan, Steuerungs-ZUSTAND - customer-grade, no buttons) and **`einrichten.html` = Einrichten** (a four-step guided commissioning flow ending at "Daten kommen an", then the areas: Wechselrichter/Erzeuger/Zähler, Portal-Kopplung, Steuerung freigeben, Messwert-Aufbereitung, Unumkehrbar). `inverter.html`/`einstellungen.html` survive ONLY as redirects into an area anchor - old URLs must never 404. A single **Technikmodus** switch (`technik.js`, same header position on both pages, `localStorage`, default OFF) reveals inline technical depth; it is NOT a gate. THE rule it encodes: **hidden content must never hide a CAUSE** - every non-ok verdict of the pure `status.js`/`control.js`/`commissioning.js` derivations carries a plain-German `cause` rendered in NORMAL mode. Details + the per-file map: `edge-app/AGENTS.md` "The `:8484` web app: TWO pages + Technikmodus".

**"Meine Anlage" (now the first AREA of Einrichten)** is one card with three ROLE groups instead of the old always-open form + flat source list: **Wechselrichter / Speicher** (`inverter.js`: a summary row with a live status dot/pill + a "Bearbeiten" toggle that reveals the model-picker form; the form is NOT permanently rendered), **Erzeuger** (N) and **Netz-Zähler** (0–1) (`sources.js`: `renderGroups()` partitions `/api/sources` by role). Adding a source opens a **drawer** (`.drawer`, side panel → full-screen sheet under `@media (max-width:640px)`) with a two-card **role picker** (`.role-pick`, replaces the old `<select id="srcRole">`; the Netz card is `disabled`/`locked` once one exists, so the 0–1 limit is visible before submit). Status pills use `shell.css` `.pill.ok/.warn/.off` (NOT the portal React `Badge`); `verify.js` (`window.VP`) holds the shared status-pill mapping + the "Verbindung testen" handler, reused by both scripts. Source rows are delete-only (no edit endpoint exists). The Go page-structure test `web_test.go TestInverterPageServesModelPickerStructure` pins the ids (`anlageCard`/`invGroup`/`erzList`/`netzList`/`srcDrawerBackdrop`/`rolePick`/`invVerify`/`srcVerify`/`invTestBtn`/`srcTestBtn`) on `einrichten.html`. The despike buttons are `#despikeSaveBtn`/`#despikeResetBtn` - the inverter form owns `#saveBtn` now that both live on one page. **`static/*` is `//go:embed`-ed — rebuild the core binary after editing** (any `go build`/`go test ./internal/web` re-embeds).
